### [1] [Natural Language Access Control (NLAC): From Help Desk Requests to Structured Policies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06726)
*Jonas Wessner,Tobias Meuser,Janek Schoffit,Dennis Eisermann,Johannes Deger,Björn Scheuermann,Frank Kargl*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于大语言模型（LLM）的自然语言访问控制（NLAC）架构，并构建了NLACBench基准测试。通过引入基于嵌入相似性的网络组件筛选与子图构建方法，成功解决了LLM在大规模网络中性能急剧下降的问题，使准确率提升至最高98.7%，同时保持资源开销恒定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在大型复杂网络中配置访问控制策略既易出错又需要大量专家工作。尽管LLM为以自然语言表达策略提供了新接口，但其将用户请求转化为访问策略的能力，以及最适合利用LLM的系统架构，目前仍缺乏深入研究。

Method: 1. 提出自然语言访问控制（NLAC）架构，使用LLM将用户请求翻译为访问策略；2. 引入NLACBench基准，用于评估大规模网络下基于LLM的意图翻译系统；3. 针对大规模网络性能下降问题，提出通过嵌入相似性识别相关网络组件，构建紧凑子图并传递给LLM，从而实现可扩展的高效推理。

Result: 1. 多个先进模型的评估显示，顶级LLM在小规模网络中可达96.9%准确率，但随着网络规模增大，性能显著下降（部分模型低于20%）；2. 所提方法使大规模网络下的准确率提升至最高98.7%，同时将推理时间、硬件需求及运营成本降低至恒定资源预算；3. 案例研究表明，顶级模型的错误模式具有互补性，提示多LLM架构有望进一步提升意图翻译准确率。

Conclusion: 本文验证了基于LLM的访问控制架构的可行性，并通过创新的子图构建方法有效解决了规模化挑战。研究还揭示了多LLM协同在提升意图翻译准确率方面的潜力，为未来网络策略配置自动化提供了新思路。

Abstract: Configuring network access control policies in large, complex networks is error-prone and requires significant expert effort. LLMs offer a promising interface for expressing such policies in natural language, but their capability for translating user requests into access policies, and the system architectures best suited to leverage LLMs, remain underexplored. We present an architecture for natural-language access control (NLAC) that uses LLMs to translate user requests into access policies, and introduce NLACBench, a benchmark for evaluating LLM-based intent translation systems in large-scale networks. Our evaluation across multiple state-of-the-art models shows that top-performing LLMs achieve up to 96.9% accuracy in small-network settings, but performance degrades substantially (below 20% for some models) as network size increases. To address this limitation, we identify relevant network components via embedding similarity and construct compact subgraphs that are passed to the LLM. This approach enables scaling to larger networks with up to 98.7% accuracy, while simultaneously reducing inference time, hardware requirements, and operating costs to a constant resource budget. Finally, a case study indicates that top-performing models exhibit largely complementary error patterns, suggesting that intent translation accuracy may be further improved through multi-LLM architectures.

</details>


### [2] [i2Slicer: Enabling Flexible and Automated Orchestration of 5G SA End-to-End Network Slices](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06955)
*M. Catalan-Cid,A. Fernandez,D. Camps-Mur,S. Siddiqui*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了i2Slicer，一个灵活的5G网络切片编排解决方案，实现了独立组网端到端网络切片的部署，支持多租户和多服务能力，并通过实验验证了其实用性和高效的切片生命周期管理能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G网络切片允许创建适应服务需求的逻辑网络，软件化推动了无需专用硬件的5G解决方案发展。垂直行业采用5G切片的关键需求是通过自动化编排简化其管理流程。

Method: 提出i2Slicer解决方案，用于编排部署5G独立组网（SA）端到端网络切片，具备多租户和多服务能力。使用最先进的5G软件和硬件平台进行实现和评估。

Result: 通过实现和评估验证，i2Slicer提供了实用且高效的网络切片生命周期管理功能。

Conclusion: i2Slicer能够有效满足垂直行业对5G网络切片简化管理的需求，为5G切片的实际部署和运营提供了可行的解决方案。

Abstract: 5G network slicing implies a step forward in customizing radio access and core networks by allowing the creation of logical networks adapted to service requirements. In addition, softwarisation has fueled the emergence of 5G solutions which do not require specialized hardware platforms. Therefore, a key requirement to drive the adoption of 5G slicing by verticals is to simplify its management through automated orchestration. In this paper, we present i2Slicer, a flexible solution to orchestrate the deployment of 5G standalone end-to-end network slices with multi-tenancy and multi-service capabilities. The implementation and evaluation of i2Slicer using state-of-the-art 5G software and hardware demonstrate that it offers a practical and efficient lifecycle management of network slices.

</details>


### [3] [DIFFRACT: Neuralized Utility Maximization for Wireless Networks by Differentiable Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07114)
*Chee Wei Tan,Siya Chen*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DIFFRACT框架，通过可微编程将深度学习与无线网络优化相结合，实现了卫星到Open RAN等下一代无线网络中的敏捷智能资源管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 下一代无线网络（包括卫星到Open RAN系统）需要敏捷且智能的资源管理机制，以应对随机服务质量约束下的动态多用户干扰问题，传统方法难以满足实时性和适应性需求。

Method: 利用标准干扰函数的数学结构发展对偶理论，通过算法展开将迭代干扰管理算法映射为可微神经网络架构，实现网络边缘的分布式端到端梯度学习，并结合可微编程将深度学习与优化问题集成。

Result: 实验结果验证了该框架的理论正确性和实际有效性，证明DIFFRACT能够支持地面和非地面环境下的实时干扰适应，实现可扩展且鲁棒的效用最大化。

Conclusion: DIFFRACT为下一代无线系统提供了一种理论完备、实践有效的资源管理解决方案，通过建模复杂信道动态并利用可微模型的表达能力，实现了智能化和自适应的无线网络优化。

Abstract: Next-generation wireless networks, including satellite-to-Open RAN systems, demand agile and intelligent resource management capable of handling dynamic multi-user interference under stochastic quality of service constraints. This paper introduces DIFFRACT, a neuralized utility maximization framework that leverages differentiable programming to integrate deep learning with optimization in wireless networks. Central to our approach is the exploitation of the mathematical structure of standard interference functions, which are foundational in wireless power control. By developing a duality theory for these functions, we map iterative interference management algorithms into differentiable neural network architectures via algorithm unrolling. This enables distributed, end-to-end gradient-based learning at the network edge, supporting real-time adaptation to interference in both terrestrial and non-terrestrial environments. DIFFRACT allows for scalable and robust utility maximization by modeling complex channel dynamics and leveraging the expressiveness of differentiable models. Experimental results confirm the framework's theoretical soundness and practical effectiveness for next-generation wireless systems.

</details>

### [4] [FP8 is All You Need (Part 1): Debunking Hardware FP64 as the HPC Holy Grail](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06510)
*Satoshi Matsuoka*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文挑战了传统HPC认为原生FP64硬件是科学计算基石的教条，提出在B300等AI优化的GPU上，利用丰富的FP8张量吞吐量结合基于中国剩余定理的Ozaki Scheme II，可以在全FP64精度下实现内存带宽限制的执行性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NVIDIA的Blackwell Ultra (B300)将原生FP64性能降至约1.3 TFLOPS，相比B200下降31倍，即使内存受限的内核（SpMV、GEMV、模板计算）也变为计算受限。传统认为原生FP64硬件是科学计算的"圣杯"，但这种设计范式在AI优化GPU上面临严峻挑战。

Method: 提出Tensor-Memory Equilibrium (TME)模型扩展Roofline模型，引入计算乘数alpha、带宽乘数beta和重构延迟gamma。通过寄存器级融合机制使beta趋近于1，结合中国剩余定理的Ozaki Scheme II在FP8张量单元上实现FP64仿真，配合Kulisch定点重构处理FFT。

Result: 在B300上实现约500 TFLOPS、在Rubin R200上实现约400 TFLOPS的仿真FP64性能，超越B200原生FP64性能一个数量级以上。相比H100基线，Ozaki II在所有研究的工作负载上匹配或超越H100性能，而B300原生FP64可能导致高达50倍的性能退化。

Conclusion: FP8配合Ozaki II和Kulisch逃逸路径足以满足生产HPC需求，原生FP64硬件不再是必需的"圣杯"。在AI优化GPU上，通过创新的仿真技术可以在全FP64精度下达到内存带宽限制的执行性能。

Abstract: Conventional HPC dogma holds that native hardware FP64 silicon is the irreducible foundation of scientific computing -- the "holy grail" of double-precision simulation. This paper argues the dogma is wrong: on AI-optimised GPUs of the B300 generation and beyond, abundant FP8 tensor throughput combined with the Chinese Remainder Theorem-based Ozaki Scheme II recovers memory-roof execution at full FP64 accuracy across the canonical HPC kernel spectrum. NVIDIA's Blackwell Ultra (B300) collapses native FP64 to ~1.3 TFLOPS -- a 31x regression from the B200 -- rendering even memory-bound kernels (SpMV, GEMV, stencils) compute-bound. We make four contributions. First, a unified analytic model, the Tensor-Memory Equilibrium (TME) model, augmenting the Roofline with a compute multiplier alpha, a bandwidth multiplier beta, and a reconstruction latency gamma. Second, we identify register-level fusion as the mechanism driving beta -> 1, making emulation essentially free behind the memory wall. Third, we project that Ozaki II vaults emulated FP64 from the ~1 TFLOPS native floor to ~500 TFLOPS (B300) and ~400 TFLOPS (Rubin R200), exceeding even B200's native FP64 ceiling by over an order of magnitude in the compute-bound regime while matching the memory roof in the bandwidth-bound regime. Fourth, against an H100 baseline, Ozaki II matches or exceeds H100 on every workload studied, versus the up-to-50x regression that B300 native FP64 imposes. Combined with a companion FFT analysis (Kulisch fixed-point reconstruction on the surviving INT32 pipe) and FP32+Kahan reductions reported in the companion Part(2) paper, every surveyed kernel class on B300 reaches the memory roof at full FP64. The evidence supports the title's claim: FP8, with Ozaki II and Kulisch escape routes, is all one needs for production HPC; native FP64 silicon is no longer the holy grail it has been taken to be.

</details>


### [5] [DxPTA: An Architecture Design Space Exploration with Optical Dataflow-guided Strategy for HW/SW Co-Design of Photonic Transformer Accelerators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06515)
*Rachmad Vidya Wicaksana Putra,Solomon Micheal Serunjogi,Mahmoud Rasras,Muhammad Shafique*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了DxPTA，一种面向光子Transformer加速器（PTA）的设计空间探索方法，可在满足面积、功耗、能量和延迟等约束条件下自动搜索最优架构，相比穷举方法快15.2倍，并能有效支持DeiT和BERT等多种Transformer模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Transformer模型虽性能优异但尺寸庞大，传统电子加速器能效受限。现有光子Transformer加速器（PTA）研究未考虑应用约束（面积、功耗、能量、延迟），且采用手动设计方法，设计时间长、不可扩展，亟需一种约束感知的自动化架构搜索方法。

Method: 提出DxPTA方法，包含三个核心步骤：（1）基于相干光学数据流识别PTA架构参数；（2）分析各参数的影响与显著性；（3）利用该分析设计约束感知的架构搜索算法，实现高效的硬件/软件协同设计。

Result: 在50mm²面积、5W功耗、50mJ能量和10ms延迟的约束条件下，DxPTA找到的最优架构可实现26mm²面积、4.8W功耗、39mJ能量和6ms延迟；同时搜索速度比穷举方法快15.2倍，成功应用于DeiT-T/S/B和BERT-B/L等多种Transformer模型。

Conclusion: DxPTA方法能够高效地为不同Transformer模型找到满足多种约束的PTA架构，显著缩短设计周期并保证性能，为面向AGI应用的多样化PTA设计提供了有效解决方案，具有重要的实用价值和推广前景。

Abstract: Transformer-based networks have emerged as prominent AI models with state-of-the-art performance, which potentially pave the way toward artificial general intelligence (AGI). However, their large sizes still hinder their efficient implementation, thus highlighting the need for alternate solutions to enable their energy-efficient acceleration. Recently, state-of-the-art works propose photonic transformer accelerators (PTAs) with significant speedup and energy efficiency improvements over the conventional electronic accelerators. However, their PTA architectures are developed without considering the application constraints (e.g., area, power, energy, and latency). Moreover, their manual design approach also requires huge design time to determine a suitable architecture for the targeted application, hence making this approach not scalable. To address these limitations, we propose DxPTA, a novel design space exploration methodology for enabling efficient hardware/software co-design of the appropriate PTA architecture that meets all constraints. It is achieved by (1) identifying the PTA architecture parameters based on the coherent optical dataflow; (2) analyzing the impact/significance of the parameters; and (3) leveraging this analysis for devising a constraint-aware architecture search algorithm. Experimental results show that, our DxPTA can find the appropriate PTA architectures for different transformer-based models (i.e., DeiT-T/S/B and BERT-B/L). It achieves up to 26mm^2 area, 4.8W power, 39mJ energy, and 6ms latency, for constraints of 50mm^2 area, 5W power, 50mJ energy, and 10ms latency; with 15.2x faster searching time than the exhaustive approach. These results demonstrate the potential of DxPTA methodology for enabling efficient PTA designs for diverse AGI-based applications.

</details>


### [6] [P-Cast Precision in FP8 Attention: Sink-Induced Collapse and the Optimality of S=2^8](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06521)
*Reed Lau*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文分析了FP8注意力计算中的精度问题，提出反向KV迭代和S=256静态缩放因子两个优化方案，解决了P-collapse现象，实现了3-10倍的MSE提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FP8 (E4M3)格式在注意力计算中能提供显著的吞吐量提升，但其3位尾数在将softmax概率矩阵P转换为FP8时引入了精度挑战，特别是在Attention Sink现象下。作者旨在理解并解决这一精度损失问题。

Method: 分析了两个影响输出精度的实现选择：(1) KV块迭代顺序，(2) 应用于P的静态缩放因子。通过理论分析推导出正向KV迭代导致P-collapse现象的闭式阈值Δc = 6.93 + lnS - δk，并提出反向迭代结合S=256的解决方案。同时给出了S=256满足IEEE 754位精确缩放、最小量化步长和最大正常范围覆盖的构造性特征。

Result: 实验表明：在中等sink强度下获得3-10倍MSE提升；反向迭代消除了P-collapse现象，当结合S=256时保证零下溢；两个优化组合后达到相同的精度上限。这些优化已部署在FlashAttention-3/4中。

Conclusion: 本研究为FlashAttention-3/4中已部署的工程优化提供了定量理论解释，建立了预测kernel级精度损失的闭式阈值，并证明了反向KV迭代和S=256静态缩放因子是数学上最优的选择。这推动了hpc-ops kernel从S=1更新到S=256。

Abstract: FP8 (E4M3) acceleration for attention computation offers significant throughput gains, but the 3-bit mantissa introduces precision challenges when the softmax probability matrix~$P$ is cast to FP8 before the $P \cdot V$ matrix multiplication. We analyze two implementation choices that affect output precision under the \emph{Attention Sink} phenomenon: (1)~the KV block iteration order, and (2) the static scaling factor applied to $P$ before casting.
  We show that forward KV iteration causes \emph{P-collapse} -- to leading order a fraction $Φ(Δ+ δ_k - 6.93 - \ln S)$ of non-sink $P$ values underflow to zero, where the small shift $δ_k \approx 1$ (for $k_{\text{sink}}{=}4$) is the expected within-sink-block score maximum -- and that reverse iteration removes it, with a zero-underflow guarantee when reverse is combined with $S{=}256$. We further give a constructive characterization of $S = 256 = 2^8$ as the static scale that simultaneously satisfies (i)~bit-exact IEEE 754 scaling, (ii) the lower envelope of a sawtooth function $dp(S)$ over the E4M3 number line ($dp = 2^{-4}$, the minimum worst-case quantization step), and (iii)~the maximum normal-range coverage \emph{among bit-exact ($2^k$) scales} (a non-bit-exact scale such as $448$ attains slightly higher coverage; sec.5}). Both optimizations are already deployed in FlashAttention-3/4 on engineering grounds; our contribution is a quantitative account of \emph{why} these choices are good and a closed-form threshold $Δ_c = 6.93 + \ln S - δ_k$ for predicting kernel-level precision loss. Kernel-faithful experiments ($Q, K, V$ in FP32 to isolate the P-cast effect) show $3$-$10\times$ MSE improvement at moderate sink strengths, and paired tests confirm both fixes saturate to the same precision floor when combined -- which motivated updating the hpc-ops kernel from $S{=}1$ to $S{=}256$.

</details>


### [7] [Ablation Study of Block Size, Weight Precision, and Scale Precision in NVFP4 Inference for Low-Power Edge-Efficient Neural Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06527)
*Ovishake Sen,Venkata Nithin Kamineni,Daniel Lobo,Swarup Bhunia,Rickard Ewetz,Baibhab Chatterjee*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出NVLUT框架，通过4位NVFP4激活值、两级缩放和LUT查表运算实现边缘设备能效提升，相比传统方案最高可降低26.85倍能耗和2.21倍面积。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘推理能效优化需要同时降低算术开销、内存流量和硬件开销。传统浮点乘法计算成本高，而现有低精度量化方法存在精度损失问题，需要一种在保持精度的同时显著降低能耗的解决方案。

Method: 提出NVLUT框架：1) NVFP4激活值格式（FP4数据 + FP8块尺度 + FP32张量尺度）；2) 将乘法分解为符号、指数、尾数三条路径，符号用XOR逻辑，指数用整数加法，尾数乘法用紧凑LUT查表替代；3) 采用电压缩放存储和选择性ECC保护；4) 系统性的消融实验分析块大小、权重精度和重训练策略。

Result: 在六个边缘高效模型上验证：1) 块大小B=16提供最佳精度/存储权衡，N=4096时仅需4.5078位/输入；2) FP8/FP16权重相比FP4权重在NVFP4激活路径下仅有适度提升；3) 相比纯无缩放FP4，无重训练的NVFP4恢复显著精度，带重训练的NVFP4达到最佳精度；4) 硬件层面相比带ECC和电压缩放的传统LUT最高降低26.85倍能耗、2.21倍面积，混合电压操作下降低22.85倍能耗、1.52倍面积。

Conclusion: NVFP4两级缩放配合选择性可靠性保护能够实现鲁棒、低能耗的边缘推理，证明了LUT量化在边缘设备上的实用价值和优越性能。

Abstract: Energy-efficient edge inference requires reducing arithmetic cost, memory traffic, and hardware overhead. This paper presents an ablation-focused study of NVFP4 LUT-based inference for edge-efficient neural networks. The proposed NVLUT framework combines 4-bit NVFP4 activations, two-level scaling, LUT-based mantissa computation, voltage-scaled storage, and selective ECC protection. Multiplication is decomposed into sign, exponent, and mantissa paths, where sign uses XOR logic, exponent uses integer addition, and mantissa multiplication is replaced by compact LUT access. NVFP4 activations use FP4 data with an FP8 block scale and an FP32 tensor scale. Across six edge-efficient models, block-size ablation shows that B = 16 offers a practical accuracy/storage trade-off, requiring only 4.5078 bits per input for N = 4096. Weight-precision ablation shows that FP8 and FP16 weights provide only modest gains over FP4 weights under the same NVFP4 activation path. Compared with pure unscaled FP4, NVFP4 without retraining recovers substantial accuracy by restoring activation dynamic range, while NVFP4 with retraining achieves the best accuracy across models. Hardware analysis shows that NVLUT achieves up to 26.85x energy reduction over traditional LUTs with ECC plus voltage scaling and up to 22.85x under mixed-voltage operation. Area is reduced by up to 2.21x and 1.52x, respectively. These results demonstrate that NVFP4 two-level scaling with selective reliability protection enables robust, low-energy edge inference.

</details>


### [8] [Quantized AI Inference on Constrained Embedded Platforms for Small-Satellite Settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06528)
*Carlos Rafael Tordoya Taquichiri,Hans Dermot Doran,Pablo Ghiglino*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文针对资源受限的小卫星场景，在高度受限的嵌入式平台（如Cortex-M）上对量化AI推理执行进行了测量表征，为有限计算和内存资源下的AI推理建立了基准参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 资源受限的小卫星环境对AI推理有严格的大小、功耗和有效载荷预算限制，这限制了星载计算能力和数据处理能力，因此需要在有限计算和内存资源下建立量化AI推理的清晰基准。

Method: 选择代表性的嵌入式视觉神经网络工作负载作为参考案例，在高度受限的嵌入式平台（如Cortex-M）上对量化执行进行基于测量的表征，将其作为下限操作点。分析了在显式编排模式下的时间行为特性，包括指令效率和内存移动的影响。

Result: 报告了延迟指标和数据移动观察结果，并结合Cortex-M上量化算术下的ALU/SIMD利用率对这些测量结果进行了解释。该表征为跨编排配置（如多核和/或多设备）估计执行时间提供了结构化参考。

Conclusion: 建立的基线为将结果与更典型的空间嵌入式处理器类别（如LEON/NOEL-V）进行对比定位提供了参考点，将编排和架构变化视为显式设计选择。

Abstract: In resource-constrained small-satellite settings, AI inference must operate under tight size, power, and payload budgets, which tend to limit onboard compute capability and data handling. These conditions motivate establishing a clear baseline for quantized AI inference under bounded compute and memory resources. To instantiate this baseline, a representative embedded-vision neural-network workload serves as the reference case. With this motivation, this paper presents a measurement-based characterization of quantized execution for this AI workload on highly constrained embedded platforms (for instance, Cortex-M), grounded as a lower-bound operating point. In this regime, scaling tends to rely on explicit orchestration rather than OS-managed, transparent multicore scheduling, and timing behavior is shaped by instruction efficiency and memory movement. As a result, the characterization provides a structured reference for estimating execution time across orchestrated configurations (e.g., multiple cores and/or devices), treating orchestration and architectural variation as explicit design choices. We report latency metrics alongside data-movement observations, and interpret these measurements in light of ALU/SIMD utilization under quantized arithmetic for the Cortex-M. Finally, we outline how this baseline provides a reference point for positioning the results against more space-typical embedded processor classes (e.g., LEON/NOEL-V).

</details>


### [9] [RTLScout: Joint Agentic Code and Synthesis Optimization for Efficient Digital Circuits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06530)
*Felix Arnold,Ryan Amaudruz,Dimitrios Tsaras,Renzo Andri,Lukas Cavigelli*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: RTLScout是一个结合LLM智能体与电路综合优化的RTL设计自动化系统，在16位浮点乘法器上实现了面积减少35%、延迟减少45%的显著改进。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统RTL设计流程依赖工程师经验，耗时且难以探索足够大的设计空间。随着LLM在代码生成方面的能力提升，作者希望利用LLM智能体自动化RTL设计优化过程，结合电路级综合优化技术，实现高质量、自主化的数字电路设计。

Method: RTLScout采用四阶段流水线：(1)智能体代码优化，LLM智能体迭代编写、评估和优化RTL代码；(2)智能体门级重写，利用工具调用进行门级优化；(3)算术架构扫描，系统化探索不同架构实现；(4)可选的高投入门级优化。系统引入多轮精英池框架，将最优设计和经验教训用于后续迭代。整个过程由Yosys和OpenROAD提供的PPA（功耗、性能、面积）反馈指导。

Result: 在支持非规格化数的IEEE-754标准16位浮点乘法器上，RTLScout相比ASAP7工艺下的初始设计，面积减少35%，延迟减少45%。每个阶段都带来显著改进，其中高投入门级优化对已优化设计效果最佳。最终Pareto前沿优于同工艺下的商业工具参考设计。

Conclusion: RTLScout成功证明了LLM驱动的智能体设计方法在RTL优化中的有效性。各阶段具有互补作用，高投入门级优化更适合作为精调阶段而非替代早期优化。该系统生成的Pareto前沿优于商业工具，展示了自主化RTL设计流程的巨大潜力。

Abstract: We present RTLScout, an autonomous system that combines LLM-driven agentic design with circuit-level synthesis optimization and arithmetic architecture sweeps. An LLM agent iteratively writes, evaluates, and refines RTL designs using tool calls, guided by quantitative PPA (power, performance, area) feedback from Yosys and OpenROAD. We introduce a multi-run elite pool framework, where the best designs and lessons learned seed subsequent agent runs. The pipeline comprises four complementary phases: agentic code optimization, agentic gate-level rewriting, arithmetic architecture sweeps, and an optional high-effort gate-level refinement pass. On an IEEE-754-compliant 16-bit floating-point multiplier with subnormal support, RTLScout reduces area by 35% and delay by 45% relative to a starting design synthesized in ASAP7 technology. Each phase provides distinct improvements, and high-effort gate-level optimization is most effective as a refinement of already well-optimized designs rather than a substitute for earlier stages. The resulting Pareto front outperforms a commercial-tool reference design on the same technology.

</details>


### [10] [MailoHLS: Multi-Adapter Structure-Aware Learning for Pareto-Driven HLS Pragma Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07246)
*Elena Vouvali,Dimosthenis Masouros,Aggelos Ferikoglou,Dimitrios Soudris,Sotirios Xydis*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了MailoHLS框架，结合LLM语义推理与GNN结构建模，实现高性能HLS指令优化，在延迟优化上达到最高12.42倍加速，显著优于现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: FPGA加速器的HLS开发面临设计空间巨大且不规则的挑战，指令选择需要考虑程序结构、内存行为和多目标权衡的复杂交互。现有模型驱动方法泛化能力有限，无法捕获高层次优化意图；而LLM虽然能捕获代码语义，但其序列化表示难以建模结构依赖和全局权衡，导致次优设计。

Method: 提出MailoHLS混合框架，将LLM语义推理与GNN结构建模相结合。通过交叉注意力机制集成结构嵌入，利用PEFT配合目标条件化LoRA适配器，并采用帕累托驱动优化，实现对代码语义、结构和设计权衡的联合推理。

Result: 在已见和未见内核上，MailoHLS分别实现最高12.42倍和8.4倍的延迟优化加速（几何平均9.48倍和4.97倍），持续生成接近帕累托最优的设计。在完全未见应用上达到最高10.2倍加速（几何平均6.58倍），优于高端LLM和现有方法，缩小了与帕累托前沿的差距。

Conclusion: MailoHLS通过结合LLM语义理解与GNN结构建模能力，成功解决了HLS指令优化中的语义-结构联合推理问题，在保证泛化性能的同时实现了接近最优的设计质量，为FPGA加速器自动化设计提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: High-Level Synthesis (HLS) enables rapid development of FPGA accelerators, yet achieving high-quality results (QoR) remains challenging due to the large and irregular design space induced by compiler directives (a.k.a pragmas). Selecting effective configurations requires reasoning over complex interactions between program structure, memory behavior, and often conflicting objectives such as latency and resource utilization. Prior model-driven approaches exhibit limited generalization across kernels and fail to capture higher-level optimization intent. Recently, Large Language Models (LLMs) capture code semantics and high-level intent, but their sequential representations hinder modeling of structural dependencies and global trade-offs, leading to suboptimal HLS designs.
  We present MailoHLS, a hybrid framework that combines LLM-based semantic reasoning with GNN-based structural modeling for objective-aware directive optimization. By integrating structural embeddings via cross-attention and leveraging PEFT with objective-conditioned LoRA adapters and Pareto-driven optimization, MailoHLS enables joint reasoning over code semantics, structure, and design trade-offs. Across seen and unseen kernels, MailoHLS achieves up to 12.42x and 8.4x speedup (9.48x and 4.97x geometric mean) for latency optimization, consistently producing near-Pareto-optimal designs. On fully unseen applications, it reaches up to 10.2x speedup (6.58x geometric mean), outperforming high-end LLMs and prior approaches while narrowing the gap to the Pareto frontier.

</details>


### [11] [A 65 nm Multi-Modal Bayesian Inference Engine with 16.3 fJ/Sample Calibration-Free GRNG for Risk-Aware At-Home Skin Lesion Screening](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07439)
*Steven Davis,Likai Pei,Jianbo Liu,Zephan M. Enciso,Boyang Cheng,Xueji Zhao,Danny Z. Chen,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 提出一种65nm风险感知多模态贝叶斯推理引擎，用于家庭环境下隐私保护的皮肤病变筛查，通过存内计算和高斯混合采样显著提升不确定性建模能力和系统鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在不受控的家庭环境中进行皮肤病变筛查需要高鲁棒性和隐私保护能力，传统单模态贝叶斯神经网络的不确定性建模能力有限，难以满足实际应用需求。

Method: 采用存内计算架构，实现字内高斯混合采样，突破了传统单模态贝叶斯网络的限制；利用互补工艺变化实现免校准高斯随机数生成，提升硬件鲁棒性。

Result: 相比最先进单模态贝叶斯神经网络：等风险操作覆盖率提升1.4倍，用户数据扰动鲁棒性提升>1.5倍，工艺变化抗性提升5.5倍，平衡准确率提升1.8%；硬件能效达16.3 fJ/样本，效率为168.6 GSa/s/mm²。

Conclusion: 该工作为隐私敏感的医疗筛查提供了一个实用、高效且风险感知的边缘AI解决方案，在家庭医疗应用场景中具有重要价值。

Abstract: We present a 65-nm risk-aware multimodal Bayesian inference engine for privacy-preserving, fully on-device skin lesion screening under uncontrolled at-home conditions. The proposed compute-in-memory architecture performs in-word Mixture-of-Gaussian sampling, improving uncertainty modeling beyond conventional unimodal Bayesian neural networks. This added probabilistic expressiveness increases equal-risk operating coverage by 1.4x, improves robustness to user-data perturbations by >1.5x, enhances process-variation resilience by 5.5x, and improves balanced accuracy by 1.8% over state-of-the-art unimodal Bayesian neural networks. Hardware robustness is further supported by calibration-free Gaussian random-number generation using complementary process variation, achieving 16.3 fJ/sample and 168.6 GSa/s/mm^2 efficiency. These results demonstrate a practical, energy-efficient, and risk-aware edge-AI solution for privacy-conscious medical screening.

</details>


### [12] [A 65 nm Trustworthy Hypoglycemia Forecasting Engine Achieving 11.3 nJ per Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07455)
*Boyang Cheng,Jianbo Liu,Pengyu Ren,Xueji Zhao,Steven Davis,Likai Pei,Zephan M. Enciso,Kai Ni,Ningyuan Cao*

Main category: cs.AR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一款基于概率决策树的65nm低功耗芯片，用于可信赖的连续血糖监测和低血糖预测，实现了11.3 nJ/推理的能效和0.825的F1分数，在噪声鲁棒性和可解释性方面显著优于传统方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 糖尿病患者需要可靠的连续血糖监测系统进行早期低血糖预警，而医疗AI系统不仅要准确和节能，还必须具备可解释性、噪声鲁棒性和不确定性感知能力，以满足医疗应用的可信赖需求。

Method: 提出一种混合架构，结合精确算术评估（用于浅层树）和基于采样的推理（用于深层树），将软决策树的复杂度从指数级降低为样本高效的遍历。设计可重构的4×24×24概率节点阵列，支持最大深度为12的任意树结构，由片上低功耗RISC-V核心协调。

Result: 在65nm CMOS工艺下流片，实现11.3 nJ/推理的能效，在连续血糖监测数据上达到最先进的30分钟预测F1分数0.825。相比传统决策树和随机森林模型，对传感器噪声和数据点丢失的鲁棒性提升4.1x至16.1x。

Conclusion: 该工作成功展示了一款节能、可解释且具备不确定性感知能力的边缘AI引擎，为可信赖的低血糖预测提供了有效的硬件解决方案，满足了医疗AI系统对准确性、能效、可解释性和鲁棒性的综合要求。

Abstract: Diabetes affects millions of people and requires reliable continuous glucose monitoring for early hypoglycemia warning. However, medical AI systems must be not only accurate and energy efficient, but also explainable, noise robust, and uncertainty aware. This work presents a 65 nm hypoglycemia forecasting engine based on probabilistic decision trees for trustworthy medical inference. The proposed hybrid architecture combines exact arithmetic evaluation for shallow tree layers with sampling based inference for deeper layers, reducing soft decision tree complexity from exponential to sample efficient traversal. A reconfigurable 4 by 24 by 24 probabilistic node array supports arbitrary tree structures with a maximum depth of 12, coordinated by an on chip low power RISC V core. Fabricated in 65 nm CMOS, the chip achieves 11.3 nJ per inference and a state of the art 30 min forecasting F1 score of 0.825 on continuous glucose monitoring data. Compared with conventional decision tree and random forest models, the proposed engine improves robustness to sensor noise and data point drop off by 4.1x to 16.1x. These results demonstrate an energy efficient, explainable, and uncertainty aware edge AI engine for trustworthy hypoglycemia forecasting.

</details>

### [13] [Detecting and Mitigating Bias by Treating Fairness as a Symmetry Operation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06514)
*Nishit Singh*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种基于对称性恢复的机器学习偏差缓解框架，通过损失函数正则化实现高达90%的违规减少，准确率损失约5%，无需因果图知识且计算轻量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在高风险社会经济环境中部署的机器学习系统普遍存在偏差问题，需要一种通用、轻量且不依赖因果图知识的公平性解决方案。

Method: 将偏差形式化为对称性破坏操作：若分类器输出在敏感属性交换（同时保持价值特征不变）的反事实操作下保持不变，则该分类器公平。采用基于损失函数的正则化作为对称性恢复机制，在四个具有不同噪声、相关性和偏差水平的合成数据集上进行评估。

Result: 该框架实现了超过90%的违规减少，准确率成本约为5%。该框架无需因果图知识，计算轻量，可泛化至任何可定义为位翻转的敏感属性。

Conclusion: 该框架适用于主流基准测试中缺乏局部歧视来源的场景，为机器学习公平性提供了一种实用且通用的解决方案。

Abstract: Machine learning systems deployed in high stakes socioeconomic settings routinely display bias. We formalize bias as a symmetry breaking operation: a classifier is fair if its outputs remain invariant under the counterfactual operation of switching a sensitive attribute, with merit features held fixed. We implement loss based regularization as a symmetry restoring mechanism and evaluate the framework on four synthetic datasets with varying levels of noise, correlation, and bias. The framework achieves upwards of 90\% violation reduction, with accuracy costs around 5\%. This framework does not require causal graph knowledge, is computationally lightweight, and generalizes to any sensitive attribute definable as a bit-flip, making it suitable for contexts where local sources of discrimination remain absent from mainstream benchmarks.

</details>


### [14] [DiBS: Diffusion-Informed Branch Selection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06518)
*Bo Liu,Yuan Xie,Yuan Gao,Xiaolong Luo,Peng Ye,Tao Chen,Fujun Han*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了DiBS方法，将扩散模型与传统符号求解器结合，通过学习到的全局引导来优化分支选择，显著减少了数独问题的搜索成本，特别是在困难实例上表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的数独求解方法存在两个互补的局限性：基于学习的方法缺乏严格的正确性保证，而完整的符号求解器在长尾搜索问题上效率较低。需要一种方法既能保证解的正确性，又能有效减少搜索成本。

Method: 提出了DiBS（Diffusion model-guided Branch Selection）方法，保持符号求解器的完整性，使用扩散模型作为分支排序的引导。核心方法是在当前部分赋值和轻量级一致性信号下对候选值进行排序，指导分支选择搜索过程。

Result: 在具有挑战性的Royle 17-线索数独基准测试上，DiBS相比强启发式基线显著减少了搜索成本，特别是在节点数、回溯次数和长尾百分位数方面。结果证实了学习到的全局引导在分支排序错误代价最高的困难实例上是有效的。

Conclusion: DiBS成功地将学习到的全局引导与完整的符号求解器相结合，既保证了解的正确性，又显著提高了搜索效率，为约束满足问题的求解提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Sudoku is a representative constraint satisfaction problem that requires global structural reasoning under strict discrete constraints. The existing works of solving Sudoku mainly focus on two dominant approaches, i.e., traditional heuristic and deep learning solver. However, they suffer from two complementary limitations: learning-based solvers lack hard correctness guarantees, while complete symbolic solvers are still prone to long-tail search. To address these shortcomings, we propose a novel diffusion model-guided approach, termed as DiBS, for the branch selection search process. Specifically, DiBS keeps the symbolic solver complete and uses the diffusion model as a branch-ordering guide. The core method is ranking candidate values under the current partial assignment and lightweight consistency signal. Furthermore, we provide an in-depth theoretical proof to reveal how it works and why it works. Experiments on the challenging Royle 17-clue Sudoku benchmark show that our DiBS substantially reduces search cost relative to strong heuristic baselines, especially in nodes, backtracks, and long-tail percentiles. Besides, these results confirm that learned global guidance is effective on hard instances where branch-order mistakes are most expensive. All codes are available at https://github.com/shanxierdan/DiBS.

</details>


### [15] [SafeGene: Reusable Adapters for Transferable Safety Alignment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06519)
*Yanghan Wang,Zhiqiang Kou,Fu Feng,Jing Wang,Xin Geng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出SafeGene，一种可复用的安全适配器模块，通过解耦安全能力与任务特定更新，解决开放权重大语言模型在下游微调后安全对齐减弱的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 开放权重大语言模型在针对特定任务微调后，安全对齐能力会减弱，即使训练数据并非恶意数据，也会导致模型更易受恶意提示攻击。随着目标模型不断更新新任务数据或用户交互，这构成了反复出现的安全恢复问题。

Method: 提出SafeGene可复用安全适配器模块，用于架构兼容模型家族内的跨任务复用。从对齐-退化模型差异中获取安全表征，通过数据感知层选择将其精炼为任务可迁移的安全向量，并通过少样本逐层系数重校准在下游任务适配模型中表达该表征。

Result: 在多个模型家族、下游任务和安全评判器上的实验表明，SafeGene增强的模型在保持下游性能的同时降低了有害响应率，在安全-效用权衡方面优于代表性安全适配方法。

Conclusion: SafeGene成功将安全能力视为独立、可复用的适配器表征，与任务特定更新解耦，为大语言模型的持续安全维护提供了一种有效解决方案，实现了更好的安全-效用权衡。

Abstract: Open-weight LLMs are increasingly fine-tuned into customized assistants, but downstream fine-tuning can weaken safety alignment and make models more vulnerable to malicious prompts, even when the training data is not intentionally harmful. This creates a recurring safety recovery problem as target models are repeatedly updated with new task data or user interactions. We propose SafeGene, a reusable safety-adapter module designed for cross-task reuse within each architecture-compatible model family. Rather than treating safety recovery as a model-specific repair step, SafeGene treats safety capability as an independent, reusable adapter representation decoupled from task-specific updates. This representation is obtained from aligned--degraded model discrepancies, refined into task-transferable safety vectors through data-aware layer selection, and expressed in each downstream task-adapted model via few-shot layer-wise coefficient recalibration. Experiments across multiple model families, downstream tasks, and safety judges show that SafeGene-enhanced models reduce harmful response rates while maintaining downstream performance, outperforming representative safe adaptation methods in safety--utility trade-off.

</details>


### [16] [Lean4Agent: Formal Modeling and Verification for Agent Workflow and Trajectory](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06523)
*Ruida Wang,Jerry Huang,Pengcheng Wang,Xuanqing Liu,Luyang Kong,Tong Zhang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 首次提出基于Lean4依赖类型形式语言的智能体框架Lean4Agent，通过形式化验证确保工作流语义一致性并定位失败，使通过验证的工作流性能提升11.94%，迭代优化后再提升7.47%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前大语言模型智能体系统缺乏形式化方法来规范、验证和调试工作流及执行轨迹，这与数学史上自然语言模糊性推动形式语言发展的挑战相似，亟需引入形式化方法来提升智能体系统的可靠性。

Method: 提出Lean4Agent框架，构建FormalAgentLib（可扩展的Lean4库）用于形式化建模和验证智能体工作流在显式假设下的语义一致性，实现执行时故障定位；并开发LeanEvolve利用验证结果迭代优化工作流。

Result: 在SWE-Bench-Verified困难子集和ELAIP-Bench子集上对5个主流LLM的实验显示：通过验证的工作流比失败工作流平均性能优11.94%；LeanEvolve进一步将SWE性能平均提升7.47%。

Conclusion: Lean4Agent开创了使用表达性依赖类型形式语言建模和验证智能体行为的新方向，为提升LLM智能体多步工作流可靠性提供了有效的形式化基础。

Abstract: Equipping Large Language Models (LLMs) to execute reliable multi-step workflows has become a central challenge in artificial intelligence. Despite recent advances in LLMs' agentic capabilities, most agent systems still lack formal methods for specifying, verifying, and debugging their workflow and execution trajectories. This challenge mirrors a long-standing problem in mathematics, where the ambiguity of natural languages (NLs) motivates the development of formal languages (FLs). Inspired by this paradigm, we propose **Lean4Agent**, to the best of our knowledge, the first framework that uses Lean4, a dependent-type FL to model and verify agent behavior. **Lean4Agent** launches **FormalAgentLib**, an extensible Lean4 library for formally modeling and verifying agent workflows' semantic consistency under explicit assumptions, and enabling localization of execution-time failures revealed by trajectories. Building on **FormalAgentLib**, we further develop **LeanEvolve**, which applies results in **FormalAgentLib** to revise workflows to enhance its capability. Extensive experiments on a hard problem subset of SWE-Bench-Verified and a subset of ELAIP-Bench across 5 leading LLMs indicate that the verification-passing workflows outperform the failing ones by an average of **11.94%**, and **LeanEvolve** further improves SWE performance by **7.47%** on average. Furthermore, **Lean4Agent** establishes a foundation for a new field of using expressive dependent-type FL to formally model and verify agent behavior.

</details>

*Sherin Muckatira,Jesse Geneson,Slava Gerovitch,Pavel Etingof,Mikhail Gronas,Anna Rumshisky*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文发布了CrowdMath数据集，包含164个专家标注的协作数学问题解决过程链。实验表明，大模型在预测下一帖子方面表现良好（83-88%准确率），但在识别贡献的功能角色方面表现较差（最佳模型macro-F1仅0.42），揭示了模型在理解协作数学推理方面存在显著差距。

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有数学推理基准主要评估定义明确的问题求解，忽视了协作式开放问题解决场景。在这种场景中，参与者提出部分论点、识别错误、修复缺陷推理，并逐步将增量贡献综合为证明。这一研究空白促使作者构建专门数据集来评估模型对协作数学推理过程的理解能力。

Method: 基于MIT PRIMES-AoPS CrowdMath项目（2016-2025）构建数据集，包含164个专家标注的进展链。每个链条追踪从开放问题陈述到完整证明的多参与者论坛讨论。帖子按其在解决方案演进过程中的功能角色进行标注（如部分进展、证明完成、错误推理、错误识别）。定义了评估任务并对六个前沿模型进行基准测试。

Result: 模型在下一帖子预测任务上达到83-88%的准确率，表明它们能够跟随数学讨论的局部流程。但在帖子角色分类任务中，最佳模型仅达到0.42的macro-F1分数，显示模型难以识别单个贡献的功能意义。

Conclusion: 研究结果揭示了现有模型在解决定义明确的数学问题与理解协作数学进展之间存在显著差距。模型能够理解讨论的局部流程，但在识别贡献的功能角色和意义方面仍有很大提升空间，这为未来研究指明了重要方向。

Abstract: Large language models have made substantial progress on mathematical reasoning, but existing benchmarks typically evaluate well-specified problems with final answers, step-by-step solutions, or complete proofs. They do not capture collaborative open-problem solving: a setting in which participants propose partial arguments, identify gaps or errors in prior steps, repair flawed reasoning, and gradually synthesize incremental contributions into a proof. We introduce CrowdMath, a dataset of 164 expert-annotated progress chains from the MIT PRIMES--Art of Problem Solving (AoPS) CrowdMath program (2016-2025), a collaborative research initiative whose discussions have led to peer-reviewed publications. Each chain traces a multi-participant forum discussion from an open-problem statement to a completed proof. Posts are labeled by their functional roles in the evolving solution process, including partial progress, proof completion, erroneous reasoning, and error identification. We define evaluation tasks and benchmark six frontier models. Models achieve 83-88% accuracy on next-post prediction, suggesting that they can follow the local flow of mathematical discussion. However, they struggle to identify the functional significance of individual contributions with the best model achieving only 0.42 macro-F1 on post-role classification. CrowdMath exposes a gap between solving well-specified mathematical problems and understanding collaborative mathematical progress as it unfolds.

</details>


### [18] [Attack Selection in Agentic AI Control Evaluations Meaningfully Decreases Safety](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06529)
*Catherine Ge-Wang,Tyler Crosse,Benjamin Hadad,Joachim Schaeffer,Ram Potham,Tyler Tracy*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 研究发现攻击者通过策略性选择攻击时机（包括何时开始攻击和何时中止攻击）可以显著降低AI控制框架的安全性评估结果，现有评估方法可能过于乐观。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI控制框架评估通常假设攻击者不会策略性地选择攻击时机，但现实中能够选择性攻击的对手更难被检测。研究旨在探究攻击选择策略对安全评估结果的影响。

Method: 将攻击选择策略分解为两部分：启动策略（决定何时开始攻击）和中止策略（决定何时中止正在进行的攻击）。在两个代理环境BashArena和LinuxArena中进行实验，在1%审计预算下测试这些策略的效果。

Result: 启动策略在BashArena和LinuxArena上均使安全性降低20个百分点；中止策略在BashArena上降低20个百分点，在LinuxArena上降低28个百分点。这些降低应被视为攻击选择效果的上限。

Conclusion: 现有控制评估可能对选择性攻击者产生过于乐观的安全估计，建议未来评估、系统卡片和安全案例应纳入攻击选择因素，以产生更现实的安全评估结果。

Abstract: An attacker that strategically chooses when to attack is much harder to catch than one that attacks indiscriminately. AI control is a safety framework for deploying capable but untrusted AI agents under the oversight of a weaker, trusted monitor and a limited human audit budget. Control evaluations stress-test these protocols by pitting a red-team attack policy against the blue-team monitor, but current evaluations typically assume attackers that do not strategically select when to attack. We study this capability, attack selection, in agentic settings by decomposing attack decisions into a start policy, which decides when an attacker should attack, and a stop policy, which decides when an attacker should abort an ongoing attack. Across two agentic settings, BashArena and LinuxArena, both policies substantially lower measured empirical safety without changing the underlying attack capability. At a 1% audit budget, our start policy reduces safety by 20pp on both BashArena and LinuxArena, and our stop policy reduces safety by 20pp on BashArena and 28pp on LinuxArena. These reductions should be interpreted as upper bounds on the effect of attack selection. Existing control evaluations may therefore yield overly optimistic safety estimates against selective attackers. We recommend that future evaluations, system cards, and safety cases elicit attack selection to produce more realistic safety estimates.

</details>


### [19] [CARVE-Q: Quantum-Proposed, Classically Certified Interactive Driving Repair](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06531)
*Yifan Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了CARVE框架，用于在自动驾驶决策否决后生成具有法律约束力的可审计修复证书，并引入CARVE-Q量子-AI搜索层来高效寻找最优修复方案，实现了量子计算与经典安全验证的融合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统在否决危险操作后，现有预测和博弈规划方法只能提供合理的合作建议，但无法证明修复方案是否遵守硬性规则、路权分配、成本分担和自身回退策略。关键问题在于：被阻止的交互是否允许一个合法、可审计且责任有界的修复方案。

Method: 1. CARVE架构：构建有限修复格（repair lattice），生成结构化证书，记录约束规则、联合修复方案、路权缩放的合作包络、责任加权成本分配和自身回退策略。

2. CARVE-Q量子-AI搜索层：针对多所有者修复导致的产品格M造成的算法瓶颈，采用Durr-Hoyer/Grover量子最小值查找算法，将查询复杂度从经典Θ(M)降至O(√M)。

3. 验证者-预言机模型：保持所有安全权威在经典计算侧，量子计算仅作为黑盒优化提议。

Result: 1. 理论证明：验证者屏蔽的证书正确性、优先级非诱导性、黑盒查询分离、有限精度可逆预言机可构造性。

2. 实验验证：在高达65,536个分配的状态向量最小值查找上成功运行；在Lanelet2基础的INTERACTION回放中实现100%路权遵守、100%责备一致性、零优先级误报。

3. 复杂度突破：量子算法相比经典算法实现了平方级查询加速。

Conclusion: 提出了"量子提议、CARVE认证"的可信量子-AI自动驾驶模式，成功将量子计算优势引入安全关键系统，同时保持经典验证的完整权威性，为认证自治系统提供了新的信任边界设计范式。

Abstract: The critical question after a correct driving veto is not only whether a maneuver is unsafe, but whether the blocked interaction admits a lawful, auditable, and responsibility-bounded repair. Prediction and game-theoretic planners can suggest plausible cooperation, yet they do not return a proof that the repair respects hard rules, right-of-way, cost allocation, and ego fallback. We introduce CARVE, Certified Affordable Repair of Vetoed maneuvers via Envelopes, a certificate architecture for prediction-free interactive repair. Given a vetoed maneuver, CARVE constructs a finite repair lattice and emits a structured certificate recording the binding rule, selected joint repair, right-of-way-scaled cooperation envelope, responsibility-weighted cost split, and ego-only fallback. This certificate view reveals the algorithmic bottleneck: multi-owner repair induces a product lattice $M = \prod_j |\mathcal{A}_j|$. We therefore introduce CARVE-Q, a verifier-shielded quantum-AI search layer that applies quantum minimum finding only to this black-box lattice while leaving all safety authority classical. In the conservative verifier-oracle model, exact classical minimum finding requires $Θ(M)$ queries in the worst case, whereas Durr-Hoyer/Grover minimum finding uses $O(\sqrt{M})$ oracle queries with high probability. We prove verifier-shielded certificate soundness, priority non-elicitation, black-box query separation, and finite-precision reversible-oracle constructibility. We then demonstrate state-vector minimum finding on CARVE repair oracles up to 65,536 assignments and validate certificate preservation on Lanelet2-grounded INTERACTION replay with 100% right-of-way respect, 100% blame consistency, and zero priority false positives. The result is a trust-bounded quantum-AI pattern for certified autonomy: quantum proposes; CARVE certifies.

</details>


### [20] [Position: Don't Just "Fix it in Post": A Science of AI Must Study Training Dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06533)
*Stella Biderman,Mohammad Aflah Khan,Niloofar Mireshghallah,Catherine Arnett,Fazl Barez,Naomi Saphra*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，主张AI科学应超越事后分析，转向研究训练动态如何产生模型行为，以实现对能力、偏见、鲁棒性和安全相关行为的预测、干预和设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI研究将模型视为静态对象，在训练后分析行为而非探究其成因。然而模型本质上是数据、目标、架构和优化动态塑造的时间演化过程的快照，因此需要建立一门研究训练动态的科学。

Method: 基于科学史和科学哲学的理论基础，阐述AI科学应满足的要求；考察机制可解释性、公平性、记忆化和简单性偏见等领域的现有进展；识别具体的开放性问题。

Result: 提出了AI科学应支持的渐进式理解形式：从早期训练信号预测结果、在轨迹偏离时进行干预、最终设计更可靠产生期望属性的训练过程。指出扩展定律已使损失预测常规化，挑战在于将其成功扩展到能力、偏见、鲁棒性和安全相关行为。

Conclusion: 建立AI科学需要从静态事后分析转向动态训练过程研究，这将推动从预测到干预再到设计的渐进式理解，为构建更可靠、安全的AI系统奠定科学基础。

Abstract: What would it mean to have a scientific understanding of AI? Models are not static objects: they are snapshots of time-evolving processes shaped by data, objectives, architectures, and optimization dynamics. Yet much of AI research treats models as fixed artifacts, analyzing behaviors after training rather than asking why they emerge. This position paper argues that a science of AI must move beyond post-hoc fixes and study the training dynamics that produce model behavior. Such a science should support progressively stronger forms of understanding: predicting outcomes from early training signals, intervening when trajectories go wrong, and ultimately designing training procedures that more reliably produce desired properties. Scaling laws have made prediction routine for loss; the challenge is extending this success to capabilities, biases, robustness, and safety-relevant behaviors. We articulate requirements for such theories grounded in the history and philosophy of science, examine progress in mechanistic interpretability, fairness, memorization, and simplicity bias, and identify concrete open problems.

</details>


### [21] [Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT): Fully Realising a GPU-based Symmetric Pseudo-Boolean SAT Solver](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06641)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出了AFSAT，一个基于连续局部搜索(CLS)的GPU加速伪布尔可满足性求解器，通过JAX编译器实现大规模并行化，在数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率方面显著优于概念验证版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 将伪布尔可满足性问题的连续局部搜索方法从概念验证阶段发展为完全工程化的求解器，解决原有方法在内存延迟、浮点表示、数值稳定性等方面的局限性，并支持单个问题实例中任意异构对称约束类型和长度的混合。

Method: 利用JAX编译器实现纯函数组合、自动向量化、自动微分和即时编译，跨候选赋值批次执行大规模并行连续局部搜索；采用定制的离散傅里叶变换实现部分解决浮点表示和稳定性限制；通过JAX数组分片实现多加速器的近线性吞吐量扩展。

Result: 相比概念验证版本，实现了数值稳定性、运行时性能和内存效率的显著提升；支持任意异构对称约束类型和长度的混合；在多加速器扩展时实现近线性吞吐量；有效解决了内存延迟和浮点表示带来的限制。

Conclusion: AFSAT成功将连续局部搜索方法工程化为实用的GPU加速伪布尔可满足性求解器，通过JAX的自动并行化和编译优化技术克服了原有方法的瓶颈，证明了该方法的可扩展性和实用性。

Abstract: We present Accelerated Fourier SAT (AFSAT), a GPU-accelerated solver for pseudo-Boolean satisfiability based on continuous local search (CLS). AFSAT realises the proof-of-concept approach, FastFourierSAT, into a fully-engineered solver supporting any heterogeneous mixture of symmetric constraint types and lengths within a single problem instance. Using the JAX compiler, AFSAT leverages pure function composition, automatic vectorisation, automatic differentiation, and just-in-time (JIT) compilation to perform massively parallel CLS across batches of candidate assignments. We demonstrate substantially improved numerical stability, runtime performance, and memory efficiency over the proof-of-concept. We achieve this by way of identifying and addressing various limitations that arise from memory latency and floating-point representation, as well as leveraging automatic parallelisation and compact representations. The inherent representational and stability limitations of floating point are partially addressed by a tailored discrete Fourier transform implementation. We achieve near-linear throughput when scaling to multiple accelerators via JAX array sharding.

</details>


### [22] [A Study of Parallel Continuous Local Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06656)
*Cody J Christopher,Charles Gretton*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文研究了并行连续局部搜索（CLS）求解对称伪布尔约束SAT问题的性能，发现冗余约束可能阻碍收敛、CLS在混合求解器中表现优异、以及局部搜索因目标函数的鞍点密集特性而快速收敛到稳定分布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代加速器硬件（如GPU）的兴起促使研究者探索并行化算法求解SAT问题。连续局部搜索（CLS）将离散的伪布尔可满足性问题松弛为连续优化问题，具有天然的并行性优势，因此需要深入研究其在SAT求解中的实际表现和局限性。

Method: 将n变量的伪布尔可满足性问题松弛为n维超立方体上的可微分连续优化问题，使可满足实例的全局最小点对应SAT问题的满足赋值。通过实证实验系统性地研究并行CLS的性能特征，包括约束冗余度的影响、混合求解器集成效果以及收敛动力学。

Result: （1）冗余约束反而可能抑制而非加速收敛；（2）CLS作为混合求解器的子求解器表现优异，能快速补全部分赋值；（3）由于目标函数鞍点密集，局部搜索快速收敛到解质量的稳定分布，后续迭代步数收益递减。

Conclusion: 研究结果为CLS在现代加速器硬件上求解SAT问题的实际应用提供了重要指导，揭示了算法设计的反直觉现象（如冗余约束的负面影响）和性能瓶颈（鞍点密集导致的收益递减），为混合求解器架构和约束预处理策略的优化提供了理论依据。

Abstract: We study parallel Continuous Local Search (CLS) as a solution approach for Boolean satisfiability problems with symmetric pseudo-Boolean (PB) constraints. Here, the $n$-variable PB-satisfiability problem is relaxed to a continuous optimisation problem with a differentiable objective function on an $n$-dimensional hypercube. For satisfiable instances, the global minimisers of this optimisation problem correspond to satisfying assignments of the SAT problem at hand. We present several novel findings via empirical experiments: (i) redundant constraints can inhibit rather than accelerate convergence; (ii) CLS shows promise as a sub-solver in hybridised settings, quickly completing partial assignments; and (iii) local search rapidly converges to a stable distribution of solution quality (i.e., degree of satisfaction), due to saddle-dense objectives where additional solver steps yield diminishing returns. Our findings inform practical uses of CLS for SAT on modern accelerator hardware.

</details>


### [23] [AEGIS: A Backup Reflex for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06660)
*Josef Chen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: AEGIS是一种选择性策略升级方法，通过轻量级探针检测弱策略的高风险步骤，仅在必要时切换到强策略，成功恢复了10.1%的失败轨迹，同时仅在38%的步骤中激活强策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 长期机器人操作任务存在渐进式失败问题：一个错误步骤会导致状态恶化，策略陷入无法恢复的困境。失败往往在实际发生前就已有可见迹象，这为提前干预提供了机会。

Method: 提出AEGIS（激活探针早期预警、门控推理切换）方法：在弱策略的冻结激活上使用轻量级探针检测高风险步骤；当探针标记某步骤时，控制权切换到更强的独立策略；仅对需要干预的步骤使用强策略，实现选择性升级。

Result: 在LIBERO-Spatial基准上，AEGIS恢复了弱策略单独会失败的10.1%轨迹，显著优于预算匹配的盲目升级（4.6%）和随机触发安慰剂（5.1%）。探针在前30%轨迹步骤中达到AUROC 0.764，仅在38%的步骤激活强策略。通过单侧精确配对McNemar检验和Holm-Bonferroni校正验证了统计显著性。

Conclusion: 时机而非计算量是解决长期机器人操作失败的关键。AEGIS通过早期预警实现精准的策略切换，在不显著增加计算成本的前提下有效提升了任务成功率。研究采用预注册分析计划，在700个公共随机数情节上验证了结果的可靠性。

Abstract: Long-horizon robot manipulation tends to fail gradually: one bad step degrades the state, and the policy spirals into a basin from which it cannot recover. The failure is often visible before it happens. We introduce AEGIS (Activation-probe Early-warning, Gated Inference Switching), a selective escalation method that uses a lightweight probe on a weak policy's frozen activations to detect high-risk steps while there is still time to act. When the probe flags a step, control switches to a stronger separate policy, but only for the steps that need it. On LIBERO-Spatial, AEGIS recovers 10.1% of the trajectories the weak policy alone loses, versus 4.6% for budget-matched blind escalation and 5.1% for a random-trigger placebo. These gains are significant under one-sided exact paired McNemar tests with Holm-Bonferroni adjustment over three pre-registered contrasts: +5.4pp over blind escalation, p=8.5e-6; +5.0pp over random triggering, p=1.0e-4; paired-trajectory bootstrap CIs exclude zero. AEGIS activates the stronger policy on only 38% of steps, so the lever is timing rather than compute. The probe clears its precondition with an early-window AUROC of 0.764, 95% CI [0.70, 0.84], read from the weak-policy path over the first 30% of trajectory steps before any handoff. We pre-register the full analysis plan, including a conditional recovered-task-rate estimand and explicit kill criteria, and confirm the result on 700 common-random-number episodes per arm, with nA-fail=646.

</details>


### [24] [A Geometric Account of Activation Steering through Angle-Norm Decomposition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06735)
*Georgii Aparin,Tatiana Gaintseva*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文通过解耦角度和径向分量的作用，重新审视了球面激活引导方法的核心假设。研究发现概念主要由角度结构表示，支持球面方法的动机，但径向分量对引导的稳定性仍有重要作用，建议将激活引导参数化为可解释的角度和径向分量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 线性激活引导虽然简单有效，但存在局限性。近期提出的球面引导方法基于“隐藏状态范数不包含概念相关信息”的假设，但这一假设尚未经过严格验证。本文旨在通过控制性实证研究，厘清角度分量和径向分量在概念表示和引导中的实际作用。

Method: 设计了一项控制性实证研究，旨在解耦隐藏状态的角度分量（与概念方向的对齐）和径向分量（隐藏状态范数）的作用。在七个语言模型上系统分析了不同引导方法如何耦合这两个几何效应，并评估它们对概念表示和下游任务的影响。

Result: 发现概念主要由角度结构表示，支持了球面方法的动机；但径向分量（范数）对引导的稳定性和下游效应仍然重要。解释了为什么具有相似概念层面效果的干预措施可能表现不同。

Conclusion: 当前将引导参数化为单一加性系数的做法混淆了角度和径向效应，建议未来的激活引导方法应明确参数化为可解释的角度和径向分量，以实现更精细的控制和更好的稳定性。

Abstract: Linear activation steering has gained popularity as a simple and empirically effective way to control language model behavior. More recently, spherical steering paradigms have been proposed to address limitations of additive interventions, often motivated by the assumption that hidden-state norm does not carry concept-relevant information. In this work, we revisit this assumption through a controlled empirical study designed to disentangle the roles of angular and radial components. We show that steering methods differ mainly in how they couple two geometric effects: changing a token's angular alignment with a concept direction and changing its hidden-state norm. Across seven language models, we find that concepts are represented primarily in angular structure, supporting the motivation for spherical methods, but that norm remains important for the stability and downstream effects of steering. Our results explain why interventions with similar concept-level effects can behave differently, and suggest that activation steering should be parameterized by interpretable angular and radial components of the intervention, rather than by a single additive coefficient that entangles these two effects.

</details>


### [25] [OpenSkill: Open-World Self-Evolution for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.06741)
*Zhiling Yan,Dingjie Song,Hanrong Zhang,Wei Liang,Yuxuan Zhang,Yutong Dai,Lifang He,Philip S. Yu,Ran Xu,Xiang Li,Lichao Sun*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OpenSkill框架，解决开放世界中智能体在无目标监督下的自我进化问题，通过从文档、代码库和网络获取知识构建可迁移技能，在多个基准测试中取得最佳自动化通过率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有自进化智能体方法依赖可用的学习闭环（如精心策划的技能、成功轨迹或验证器信号），但真实开放世界部署可能仅提供任务提示，缺乏这些监督信号。需要研究如何在无目标任务监督下，让智能体从零开始构建技能和验证信号。

Method: 提出OpenSkill框架，通过三个步骤构建自我进化闭环：(1)从文档、代码库和网络获取基础知识和验证锚点；(2)将这些知识综合为可迁移的技能；(3)基于自建的虚拟任务（以锚点而非目标答案为基础）对这些技能进行精炼。开放世界同时提供学习知识和独立于监督的实践环境。

Result: 在三个基准测试和两个目标智能体上，OpenSkill在满足无监督约束的条件下实现了最佳自动化通过率。技能可在不同模型间迁移而无需特定模型适配；自建验证器虽从未访问真实结果，但与真实结果高度一致。

Conclusion: OpenSkill成功实现了开放世界中的无监督自我进化，证明了智能体可以在缺乏目标监督的情况下，利用开放世界资源构建有效的技能和验证机制，且这些成果具有良好的跨模型泛化能力。

Abstract: Self-evolving agents requires adaptation after deployment, but existing approaches assume a usable learning loop, such as curated skills, successful trajectories, or verifier signals. Real open-world deployments may provide none of these, offering only a task prompt. In this work, we study open-world self-evolution, where an agent must build both its skills and its own verification signals from scratch, using open-world resources but no target-task supervision. We propose OpenSkill, a framework that bootstraps this loop: it acquires grounded knowledge and verification anchors from documentation, repositories, and the web, synthesizes them into transferable skills, and refines those skills against self-built virtual tasks grounded in the anchors rather than in target answers. The open world thus supplies both the knowledge to be learned and a supervision-independent practice environment, with target-task supervision reserved for final evaluation. Across three benchmarks and two target agents, OpenSkill attains the best automated pass rate while satisfying the no-supervision constraint. Analysis shows its skills transfer across models without model-specific adaptation, and its self-built verifier aligns with ground-truth outcomes despite never accessing them.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大语言模型（LLM）作为工具使用智能体在长周期任务中受限，主要面临记忆、组织和重用知识的挑战。现有记忆方法主要关注事实信息存储，而近期程序记忆工作虽有进展，但往往仅重放成功案例，缺乏对失败情况的处理和在线可扩展性。

Method: 提出了一个统一自动的记忆框架，采用双层设计整合短期和长期存储，融合语义、情景和程序三种记忆类型。通过包含行动者、记忆和评论者的多智能体架构实现自动记忆生成、奖励标注和自适应检索。长期记忆通过基于奖励的评估、合并和剪枝进行管理，确保可扩展性和持续改进。

Result: 在多种环境下的实验表明，该方法在长多轮任务中相比现有基线方法提升了鲁棒性和成功率。

Conclusion: 强调了综合性、适应性记忆对推进基于LLM智能体发展的重要性，为构建更强大的智能体系统提供了有效的记忆管理解决方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) show promise as tool-using agents but remain limited in long-horizon tasks that require remembering, organizing, and reusing knowledge. Prior memory approaches aim to resolve the situation, but mainly focus on storing factual information. Recent work on procedural memory improves task reuse, yet often reduces to replaying past successes without addressing failure cases or online scalability. We introduce a unified and automatic memory framework that integrates semantic, episodic, and procedural memory in a bi-level design combining short-term and long-term stores. A multi-agent architecture with actor, memory, and critic agents enables automatic memory generation, reward annotation, and adaptive retrieval. Long-term memory is managed through reward-based evaluation, merging, and pruning, ensuring scalability and continual improvement. Experiments across various environments show that our approach improves robustness and success on long multi-turn tasks compared to existing baselines. This work highlights the importance of comprehensive, adaptive memory for advancing LLM-based agents.

</details>
