Extensive experiments across diverse domain-specific benchmarks demonstrate that SETA achieves competitive or superior overall performance relative to state-of-the-art continual learning baselines, with particularly strong retention of early-task knowledge and improved backward transfer on LLaMA-2 7B and Qwen3-4B.

</details>


### [134] [Structure-Preserving Correction Learning for Sparse Bayesian Inference in Brain Source Imaging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07196)
*Marco Morik,Xiao Ruiting,Shinichi Nakajima,Stefan Haufe,Ismail Huseynov*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种结构化校正学习框架，通过展开经典的联合超参数学习求解器为可训练的神经网络架构，在保留贝叶斯推理可解释性的同时提升了M/EEG脑成像的源重建性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的稀疏Type-II贝叶斯方法用于M/EEG脑成像时，虽然支持源和噪声超参数的联合估计，但依赖固定的迭代更新规则。这些更新规则虽然有原则且可解释，但其动态特性无法从数据中自适应学习，限制了性能提升空间。

Method: 将经典的联合超参数学习求解器展开为可训练的神经网络架构，网络层结构镜像原始迭代过程。框架初始化时可精确恢复经典求解器，通过渐进式的校正学习机制进行增强，包括可学习偏置、自适应MLP和基于注意力的上下文细化机制。训练过程不替换贝叶斯推理，而是学习结构化的校正项，保留原始更新动态的可解释性和模型驱动特性。

Result: 实验结果表明，所学习的校正变体在重建性能和收敛行为上均优于基线展开求解器，同时保持了算法的透明性。该方法在提升经验重建性能的同时成功保留了原始基于模型的推理机制。

Conclusion: 结构化校正学习提供了一种有效的途径来改进经典贝叶斯方法的性能，同时保留其模型驱动的可解释性本质，为传统迭代优化方法与现代深度学习的结合提供了新的思路。

Abstract: Classical sparse Type-II Bayesian methods for M/EEG brain imaging support joint estimation of source and noise hyperparameters, but rely on fixed iterative update rules. Although these updates are principled and interpretable, their dynamics cannot be adapted from data. We propose to learn the update mechanism itself while preserving the underlying Bayesian structure by unfolding a classical joint hyperparameter-learning solver into a trainable neural architecture whose layers mirror the original iterations. The resulting framework is initialized to recover the classical solver exactly before training and is enriched through progressively more expressive correction-learning mechanisms, ranging from learnable biases to adaptive MLP and attention-based contextual refinements. In this way, training does not replace Bayesian inference with a black-box predictor, but instead learns structured correction terms while retaining the interpretability and model-based character of the original update dynamics. Structured correction learning therefore aims to improve empirical reconstruction performance without replacing the original model-based inference mechanism. Experimental results show that the learned correction variants improve reconstruction performance and convergence behavior over the baseline unfolded solver while preserving its algorithmic transparency.

</details>


### [135] [Generative Molecular Morphing for Flexible-Size Design via Unbalanced Optimal Transport](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07239)
*Malte Franke,Stefan P. Schmid,Zarko Ivkovic,Kjell Jorner,Andreas Krause*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Morph，一种支持动态尺寸的3D分子生成模型，解决了现有固定尺寸模型无法准确捕捉分子性质与原子数量联合分布的问题，实现了更灵活的分子设计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有基于扩散和流的分子生成模型固定原子数量，无法准确捕捉分子性质与分子尺寸之间的内在关联，限制了模型向高回报样本导航的能力。

Method: 提出Morph，一种基于几何图的灵活尺寸生成模型，能够动态调整分子尺寸，并无缝整合现有结构先验（如骨架），支持条件和无条件3D分子生成。

Result: Morph在性能上匹配当前固定尺寸的最先进模型，同时具备更强的采样灵活性；在先前模型失败的分布外生成场景中表现出色。

Conclusion: Morph通过动态适应尺寸显著提升了分子性质导航能力，为分子设计的生成建模开辟了新路径。

Abstract: The success of generative molecular design hinges on a model's steerability toward high-reward samples. Because many molecular properties are intrinsically linked to molecular size, accurately capturing the joint distribution of properties and the number of atoms is essential. However, current diffusion and flow-based models fix the number of atoms, which ultimately limits their ability to navigate this complex relationship. To address this, we introduce Morph, a flexible-size generative model for conditional and unconditional 3D molecular design based on geometric graphs. By dynamically adapting size, Morph can seamlessly integrate existing structural priors, like scaffolds, and significantly enhances property steering. We show that Morph matches current fixed-size state-of-the-art models while offering the benefit of unparalleled sampling flexibility. We demonstrate out-of-distribution generation in regimes where previous models fail, paving the way for enhanced generative modeling for molecular design.

</details>


### [136] [A Held-Out Transition-Pair Falsifier for Long-Horizon Non-Abelian State Tracking](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07254)
*Jeonghoon Lee*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: Summary generation failed


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Motivation analysis unavailable

Method: Method extraction failed

Result: Result analysis unavailable

Conclusion: Conclusion extraction failed

Abstract: State tracking exposes a sharp limitation of sequence models: the relevant signal is often not a summary of observed tokens, but an ordered latent state that evolves through non-commutative transformations. We introduce a held-out transition-pair falsifier for finite non-Abelian group tracking. The protocol forbids selected ordered generator pairs during training and requires the same local patterns during evaluation, blocking one direct local-transition memorization pathway. In a controlled $S_3 \times S_3$ benchmark, a projected recurrent state model trained only on length-8 sequences produces error-free final-state predictions (perfect 250/250 per horizon) through evaluation horizons up to 1,048,576 tokens across five seeds. Matched native-readout baselines, including bag, GRU, and a single-configuration structured state-space model, remain near floor under the same protocol. Projection-matched GRU, structured SSM, and bag baselines equipped with analogous finite-group prototype readouts also remain near chance under the same split. Mechanism diagnostics show that hard projection coincides with low homomorphism error, low state-consistency drift, and non-trivial commutator separation, while softened projection collapses final-state accuracy. Clean-split audits verify zero verbatim reduced-word overlap and zero structural-template overlap between training and evaluation partitions. The evidence is scoped to this controlled finite-group falsifier rather than to a general architecture ranking. Within that regime, explicit projected non-commutative state composition acts as a useful inductive bias for long-horizon hidden-state tracking.

</details>


### [137] [Closed-Form Spectral Regularization for Multi-Task Model Merging](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07289)
*Yongxian Wei,Runxi Cheng,Xingxuan Zhang,Li Shen,Chun Yuan,Peng Cui,Dacheng Tao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出SWUDI和SWUDI-A两种闭式谱滤波方法用于多任务模型合并，在保持或超越SOTA性能的同时，将计算时间减少28-72倍，GPU内存降低达50%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有SOTA模型合并方法将合并建模为逐层二次干扰最小化问题，虽然存在闭式伪逆解，但实际性能不如迭代梯度下降。迭代求解器主导了计算成本，但其有效性机制一直未被解释。

Method: 作者首先揭示迭代求解器实际上充当了不适定正规方程的隐式谱正则化器，抑制了小特征值方向上的噪声放大。基于此发现，将多任务模型合并形式化为噪声线性逆问题，提出谱滤波估计器。具体实现SWUDI方法：结合软指数滤波（匹配迭代下降的梯度流轨迹）和硬性top-K截断（抑制噪声放大小特征值方向）。进一步提出自适应变体SWUDI-A，用逐层秩规则替代全局秩超参数。两者仅需每线性层一次对称特征分解，无需训练数据或优化器状态。

Result: 在四个通用基准和一个多模态合并基准（涵盖VQA、几何、图表、OCR、定位和模态合并）上，所提谱求解器匹配或超越SOTA合并方法。关键地，计算时间减少28-72倍，峰值GPU内存降低达50%。

Conclusion: 该工作从理论上揭示了迭代求解器在模型合并中的隐式正则化作用，据此提出的闭式谱滤波方法在性能和效率上均显著优于现有迭代方法，为模型合并提供了理论与实用价值兼具的新范式。

Abstract: Model merging combines several independently fine-tuned experts into a single multi-task model without any training data, reducing the storage, serving, and decentralized-development costs of large foundation models. State-of-the-art merging methods formulate merging as a layer-wise quadratic interference minimization problem. Although this problem admits an exact closed-form pseudoinverse solution, that solution underperforms hundreds of iterations of gradient descent in practice. The iterative loop dominates the cost of the pipeline, yet its effectiveness has remained unexplained. We revisit this regime and show that the iterative solver does not primarily act as an optimizer; rather, it serves as an implicit spectral regularizer for an ill-posed normal equation, where small-eigenvalue directions of the per-layer interference operator amplify proxy noise. Building on this finding, we formalize multi-task model merging as a noisy linear inverse problem and propose a spectral filtering estimator parameterized by a per-direction filter. We instantiate this estimator with SWUDI, a closed-form method that combines a soft exponential filter, which matches the gradient-flow trajectory of iterative descent, with a hard top-K truncation that suppresses noise-amplifying small-eigenvalue directions. Furthermore, we propose SWUDI-A, an adaptive variant that replaces the global rank hyperparameter with per-layer rank rules, further improving robustness across architectures. Both variants share a single symmetric eigendecomposition per linear layer and require no training data or optimizer state. Across four general benchmarks and a multimodal merging benchmark spanning VQA, Geometry, Chart, OCR, Grounding, and modality merging, our proposed spectral solvers match or outperform state-of-the-art merging methods. Crucially, they reduce wall-clock time by 28-72x and peak GPU memory by up to 50%.

</details>


### [138] [Trio: Learning Time-Series Forecasting with Temporal-Spatial-Sample Attention and Structural Causal Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07291)
*Tao Chen,Yexu Zhou,Zhi Gong,Hengwei He,Hongda Li,Zhewei Chen,Dongjing Wang,Xin Zhang,Decheng Liu,Chunlei Peng,Zheng Chen,Wenyue Ding*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Trio架构，通过时间-空间-样本注意力机制实现样本感知的时间序列预测，并引入TS-SCM生成器创建合成预测任务，在多个基准上提升了预测性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多变量时间序列预测需要模型推理时间动态、跨变量依赖和历史输入输出对应关系。虽然先验数据拟合网络（PFNs）表明合成任务有助于学习可迁移的推理行为，但直接将该范式转移到时间序列预测仍然困难，因为时间顺序、动态滞后和周期性历史模式无法被普通表格先验自然捕获。

Method: 提出Trio架构，包含三种注意力机制：时间注意力捕获窗口内动态，空间注意力建模变量间依赖，样本注意力检索相关的历史回顾-预测对来指导当前预测。同时引入时间序列结构因果模型（TS-SCM）生成器，创建具有动态滞后、跨变量交互、噪声、反馈和分布漂移的结构化合成预测任务。

Result: 在合成、工业和公共基准数据集上的实验表明，所提架构提升了预测性能。探索性零样本实验进一步表明，TS-SCM生成的任务可以提供有用的结构先验。

Conclusion: 本研究探讨了如何在预测模型中显式组织和重用历史输入输出示例，证明了样本注意力机制的有效性。然而，完全通用的PFN式时间序列预测仍然是一个开放问题，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Multivariate time-series forecasting requires models to reason over temporal dynamics, cross-variable dependencies, and historical input-output correspondences. Recent Prior-Data Fitted Networks (PFNs) suggest that synthetic tasks can be useful for learning transferable inference behavior. However, directly transferring this paradigm to time-series forecasting remains difficult, since temporal order, dynamic lags, and recurring historical patterns are not naturally captured by ordinary tabular priors. Motivated by this observation, we propose Trio, a sample-aware time-series forecasting architecture based on Temporal-Spatial-Sample attention. Temporal attention captures within-window dynamics, spatial attention models inter-variable dependencies, and sample attention retrieves relevant historical lookback-future pairs to guide the current prediction. Rather than claiming a fully general PFN-style forecaster, our goal is to study how historical input-output examples can be explicitly organized and reused within a forecasting model. We further introduce a Time-Series Structural Causal Model (TS-SCM) generator to create structured synthetic forecasting tasks with dynamic lags, cross-variable interactions, noise, feedback, and distributional drift. Experiments on synthetic, industrial, and public benchmarks show that the proposed architecture improves forecasting performance. Exploratory zero-shot experiments further suggest that TS-SCM-generated tasks may provide useful structural priors, while fully general PFN-style time-series forecasting remains an open problem.

</details>


### [139] [Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence: Explanatory Insufficiency as a Driver of Representation Learning and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07303)
*Jacques Raynal,Pierre Slangen,Elsa Raynal,Jacques Margerit*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了表示涌现的自举理论（TBER），阐述了新表示如何在现有表示解释力不足时产生，将解释不足视为表示转换的积极信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前研究多关注表示框架选定后的优化问题，而忽视了何时需要引入新的表示层级。现有表示可能仍能描述观察结果，但无法使其组织或变换变得可理解，这种解释差距驱动了表示创新的必要性。

Method: 提出了表示涌现的自举理论（TBER）框架，通过五个阶段形式化表示转换过程：稳定观察、异常检测、识别解释不足、表示涌现、临时稳定。该过程遵循递归序列：观察揭示异常→异常暴露不足→不足激发新表示→新表示产生进一步观察和新的可能不足。

Result: TBER框架成功应用于表示学习、潜在空间、基础模型、世界模型、数字孪生、自适应生物系统和科学发现等多个领域。理论表明表示不足并非因其错误，而是因其解释域被超越。

Conclusion: 未来AI系统可能受益于能够检测自身内部表示解释极限的机制。表示创新不仅由更多数据、更大模型或更强算力驱动，更由持续的解释差距驱动，这为AI系统的发展提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: Representation learning is central to modern machine learning, enabling transitions from handcrafted features to learned embeddings, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, and digital twins. Yet most research examines how representations are optimized after a representational framework has been selected, while less attention is given to when a new level of representation becomes necessary. We introduce the Bootstrap Theory of Representational Emergence (TBER), a framework describing how new representations arise when existing ones become explanatorily insufficient. In this view, representational innovation is not only driven by more data, larger models, or greater computational power, but also by persistent explanatory gaps: situations in which a representation can still describe observations but can no longer make their organization or transformations intelligible. TBER identifies explanatory insufficiency as a positive signal for representational transition. A representation becomes insufficient not because it is necessarily false, but because its explanatory domain has been exceeded. The bootstrap dynamic follows a recursive sequence: observations reveal anomalies; anomalies expose insufficiencies; insufficiencies motivate new representations; and these new representations generate further observations and possible new insufficiencies.We formalize this process through five stages: stabilized observation, anomaly detection, recognition of explanatory insufficiency, representational emergence, and provisional stabilization. We discuss applications to representation learning, latent spaces, foundation models, world models, digital twins, adaptive biological systems, and scientific discovery. TBER suggests that future AI systems may benefit from mechanisms for detecting the explanatory limits of their own internal representations.

</details>


### [140] [TabSwift: An Efficient Tabular Foundation Model with Row-Wise Attention](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07345)
*Si-Yang Liu,Han-Jia Ye*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了TabSwift，一种轻量级的表格基础模型，通过门控注意力稳定机制和可学习的寄存器令牌两个简单增强，在保持竞争力的同时显著降低了推理成本，并支持分类和回归任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前的表格基础模型（如TabPFN）虽然在中小型数据集上表现良好，但为了提高准确率，往往采用越来越复杂的架构，导致推理成本增加，限制了实际部署。因此，需要一种既能保持竞争力又能高效推理的轻量级解决方案。

Method: 提出TabSwift模型，采用轻量级的行级纯注意力骨干网络，并引入两个简单增强：(1)门控注意力稳定机制；(2)少量可学习的寄存器令牌，提供全局上下文并提升预训练质量。此外，针对延迟敏感场景，设计了自适应的层级提前退出机制，根据样本动态调整推理深度。

Result: TabSwift在分类和回归任务上与更强的表格基础模型（如TabPFN v2和TabICL）表现相当，同时推理效率更高。自适应提前退出机制进一步支持延迟敏感的服务场景。

Conclusion: TabSwift成功实现了高效且支持随时退出的表格上下文学习，适用于实际部署场景。研究表明，轻量级架构配合简单有效的增强机制，可以在不牺牲性能的前提下显著提升推理效率。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models, exemplified by TabPFN, perform prediction via in-context learning, inferring test labels directly from labeled training examples. They have demonstrated competitive performance, particularly on small-to-medium datasets. However, recent tabular foundation models often improve accuracy with increasingly complex architectures, incurring higher inference cost and limiting practical deployment. In this work, we revisit the original TabPFN design and show that a lightweight row-wise attention-only backbone can remain highly competitive with two simple enhancements: a gated attention stabilization mechanism and a small set of learnable register tokens that provide global context and improve pretraining quality. The resulting model, TabSwift, supports both classification and regression, and is competitive with stronger tabular foundation models (e.g., TabPFN v2 and TabICL) while being more efficient at inference. For latency-sensitive serving, we further introduce an adaptive layer-wise early-exit mechanism that dynamically adjusts inference depth per sample. Overall, TabSwift enables efficient and anytime tabular in-context learning for practical deployments.

</details>


### [141] [Breaking the Ice: Analyzing Cold Start Latency in vLLM](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07362)
*Huzaifa Shaaban Kabakibo,Animesh Trivedi,Lin Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文首次对vLLM推理引擎的启动延迟进行了系统性性能分析，将其分解为六个基础步骤，发现主要受CPU限制，并开发了轻量级分析模型来准确预测启动延迟，为大规模推理环境的资源规划提供指导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着可扩展推理服务的普及，推理引擎的冷启动延迟变得至关重要。vLLM已成为许多推理工作负载的事实标准选择，但由于其复杂性和快速演进，缺乏对其启动延迟的系统性研究。特别是在引入V1 API和torch.compile等重大架构创新后，亟需对其启动延迟进行详细的性能表征。

Method: 将vLLM启动过程分解为六个基础步骤进行详细分析；通过实验展示每个步骤相对于模型级和系统级参数的一致且可解释的扩展趋势；实现延迟源的细粒度归因；基于分析洞察开发轻量级分析模型，用于预测给定硬件配置下的vLLM启动延迟。

Result: 证明vLLM启动过程主要受CPU限制；每个启动步骤展现出与模型级和系统级参数一致且可解释的扩展趋势；开发的轻量级分析模型能够准确预测vLLM启动延迟；提供了针对大规模推理环境资源规划的可操作指导。

Conclusion: vLLM启动延迟具有可预测性和可解释性，通过系统性分析和建模，可以为大规模推理服务部署提供有效的资源规划指导。所有基准测试数据集、分析工具和预测脚本已开源。

Abstract: As scalable inference services become popular, the cold start latency of an inference engine becomes important. Today, vLLM has evolved into the de facto inference engine of choice for many inference workloads. Although popular, due to its complexity and rapid evolution, there has not been a systematic study of its startup latency. With major architectural innovations such as the V1 API and the introduction of torch.compile, this paper presents the first detailed performance characterization of vLLM startup latency. We break down the startup process into six foundational steps and demonstrate that it is predominantly CPU bound. Each step exhibits consistent and interpretable scaling trends with respect to model-level and system-level parameters, enabling fine-grained attribution of latency sources. Building on these insights, we develop a lightweight analytical model that accurately predicts vLLM startup latency for a given hardware configuration, providing actionable guidance for resource planning in large-scale inference environments. All benchmarking datasets, analysis tools, and prediction scripts are open sourced at https://github.com/upb-cn/vllm-startup-profiler.

</details>


### [142] [Self-evolving LLM agents with in-distribution Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07367)
*Yudi Zhang,Meng Fang,Zhenfang Chen,Mykola Pechenizkiy*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Q-Evolve框架，通过统一自动过程奖励标注和策略学习，在分布内强化学习范式中实现LLM代理的自我进化，有效解决了长序列决策中的信用分配问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型在复杂环境中作为交互式代理时，长序列决策的可靠训练仍是根本挑战。核心困难在于信用分配问题：代理通常只在回合结束时获得延迟奖励，缺乏细粒度的过程监督信号。

Method: 提出Q-Evolve自我进化框架：(1)从混合离策略数据集（专家演示+代理生成轨迹）学习分布内评论器，通过加权Implicit Q-Learning目标稳定稀疏奖励设置下的Bellman备份；(2)利用学习到的价值函数通过优势估计推导逐步过程奖励，无需环境回溯或人工标注；(3)执行行为近端策略优化，在用于过程奖励标注的数据上演化代理，避免加剧分布偏移。

Result: 在AlfWorld、WebShop和ScienceWorld三个基准上的实验表明，Q-Evolve在样本效率、鲁棒性和整体任务性能方面均优于强基线方法，证明了通过过程级监督与策略的共同演化可实现稳定的代理自我进化。

Conclusion: 通过在共享的分布内学习循环中共同演化过程级监督和策略，可以稳定地实现LLM代理的自我进化，为解决长序列决策中的信用分配问题提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) have recently emerged as powerful controllers for interactive agents in complex environments, yet training them to perform reliable long-horizon decision making remains a fundamental challenge. A key difficulty lies in credit assignment: agents often receive delayed rewards only at the end of episodes. In this paper, we propose Q-Evolve, a self-evolving framework for LLM agents that unifies automatic process-reward labeling and policy learning within a principled in-distribution reinforcement learning paradigm. In each evolving iteration, our method learns an in-distribution critic from a hybrid off-policy dataset that combines expert demonstrations with agent-generated trajectories, stabilizing Bellman backups in sparse-reward settings via a weighted Implicit Q-Learning objective. The learned value function is then used to derive step-wise process rewards through advantage estimation, enabling dense and reliable supervision without environment backtracking or human annotation. Leveraging these signals, we perform behavior-proximal policy optimization that evolves the agent over the data used for process reward labeling, allowing iterative self-improvement without exacerbating distribution shift. We evaluate our method on AlfWorld, WebShop, and ScienceWorld, showing Q-Evolve outperforms strong baselines in sample efficiency, robustness, and overall task performance. Our results demonstrate that stable agent self-evolution is achievable through the co-evolution of process-level supervision and policy, both grounded within a shared in-distribution learning loop.

</details>


### [143] [Covariance Shrinkage via Stochastic Interpolation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07382)
*Mathieu Chalvidal,Florentin Coeurdoux,Eric Vanden-Eijnden*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文将高维协方差矩阵的经典收缩估计重述为参数化随机插值问题，揭示了调度、流映射与耦合、早停三种降低统计风险的机制，提出了基于神经网络的插值估计器并验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的高维协方差收缩估计方法缺乏统一的理论框架，且未能充分探索降低统计风险的所有可能机制。作者希望将这些方法统一到经验风险最小化的框架下，并发现新的风险降低途径。

Method: 将协方差收缩估计重构为源分布与目标分布之间的参数化随机插值问题。该形式化框架揭示了三种风险降低机制：调度（插值调度决定可达到的风险下界）、流映射与耦合（通过最优传输等特定耦合结构和非线性流实现特征向量正则化）、早停（通过近似真实插值分布实现偏差-方差权衡）。进而提出了基于神经网络的插值估计器，并推导了其二次风险上界。

Result: 理论分析表明该框架能够恢复已知的收缩估计器作为特例；特定的耦合结构（如最优传输解）和非线性流映射能够降低经验风险；神经网络插值估计器的二次风险具有以插值逼近误差为界的上界。在合成实验中验证了理论结果，在真实神经影像数据上展示了额外的正则化能力。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一个统一的理论框架，不仅涵盖了经典的收缩估计方法，还揭示了新的风险降低机制。所提出的神经网络估计器在实践中展现出优越的正则化性能，为高维协方差估计提供了新的方法论途径。

Abstract: We recast classical shrinkage of high-dimensional covariance estimators as empirical risk minimization over a parametric stochastic interpolant between a source and a target distribution. This formalism recovers known shrinkage estimators as special cases and reveals three distinct mechanisms for reducing statistical risk: (i) Scheduling: the interpolant schedule determines the class of admissible covariances, and hence the achievable risk. (ii) Flow maps and couplings: whereas naive constructions amount to assuming independence between the distributions, specific coupling structures (e.g., solutions of optimal transport problems) can lower the empirical risk. Moreover, non-linear flow maps realizing such couplings free the interpolant covariance from the eigenbasis of the empirical estimate, enabling eigenvector regularization. (iii) Early stopping: estimators defined by integrating a regressed vector field afford an additional bias-variance trade-off through approximation of the true interpolant distribution. We then propose a neural estimator of the interpolant, together with an upper bound on its quadratic risk in terms of the interpolant approximation error, and validate both on synthetic experiments. Finally, we apply the estimator to real neuroimaging data, demonstrating the additional regularization power this approach offers in practice.

</details>


### [144] [Making the Most of Limited Data: Score-Aware Training for Text-to-Music Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07387)
*Yun-Chen Cheng,Tzu-Hung Huang,Chih-Pin Tan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了一种名为score-aware training的文本到音乐生成训练方法，通过重新利用低质量数据段并优化训练流程，在450M参数规模下实现了优异性能，在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge效率赛道中获得客观评估第2名和MOS评估第3名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有最先进的文本到音乐生成系统依赖大规模专有数据集和工业级计算资源，难以区分架构设计的贡献与资源优势的贡献。研究旨在在有限资源条件下，通过创新的训练策略提升模型性能，而非单纯依赖数据和算力堆叠。

Method: 提出score-aware training方法：将音频-字幕对齐分数作为直接监督信号；使用CLAP条件化的Beta噪声时间步调度，将低分数据段路由到高噪声训练区域作为隐式正则化；结合片段级过滤移除最不匹配样本；采用两阶段字幕生成流程弥合训练与推理的分布差距；引入REPA辅助损失从预训练CLAP和MuQ编码器迁移语义知识。

Result: 基于FluxAudio的450M参数系统在ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge效率赛道中，客观评估在两个赛道均排名第2，最终MOS评估在效率赛道排名第3。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了通过创新的训练策略（score-aware training）可以有效利用低质量数据，在不依赖大规模资源和参数的情况下实现竞争力强的文本到音乐生成性能，为资源受限场景下的高质量音频生成提供了可行方案。

Abstract: State-of-the-art text-to-music generation systems rely on massive proprietary datasets and industrial-scale compute, making it impossible to disentangle architectural contributions from resource advantages. We propose \textit{score-aware training}, which treats audio-caption alignment score as a direct supervision signal throughout the pipeline. Rather than discarding low-scoring segments, we repurpose them via a CLAP-conditioned Beta noise timestep schedule that routes them to high-noise training regimes, acting as an effective implicit regularizer. Complementarily, segment-level filtering removes the most misaligned examples, and a two-stage caption procedure bridges the distribution gap between verbose training captions and concise inference prompts. A REPA auxiliary loss further transfers structured semantic knowledge from pretrained CLAP and MuQ encoders without additional data. Our 450M-parameter FluxAudio-based system, submitted to the ICME 2026 ATTM Grand Challenge Efficiency Track, ranked 2nd across both tracks in the objective evaluation and 3rd in the Efficiency Track in the final MOS evaluation.

</details>


### [145] [Generative Modeling of Discrete Latent Structures via Dynamic Policy Gradients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07400)
*Stefan Ivanovic,Ge Liu,Mohammed El-Kebir*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了GReinSS框架，通过动态调整奖励的策略学习方法，解决了从间接观测中推断组合潜在状态的难题，在模拟数据和真实RNA测序数据上均表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多科学问题需要从间接观测中推断未观测的机制性潜在状态。经典方法（如期望最大化）无法扩展到组合空间，而深度学习方法（如变分自编码器）通常形成人工潜在状态而非重构真实的机制性状态。

Method: 引入GReinSS（Generative Reinforcement of State Structure），这是一个策略学习框架，使用动态重新调整的奖励来学习潜在状态分布，以最大化观测数据的似然。

Result: GReinSS能够准确重构模拟的潜在集合和潜在图，性能优于其他策略学习和生成建模基线方法。在真实短读长RNA测序数据上重构的异构体与正交长读长测序检测到的异构体匹配度优于标准RSEM算法。

Conclusion: GReinSS是从间接观测中进行组合潜在状态的生成建模和推断的一种有原则且实际有效的方法。

Abstract: Many scientific problems require inferring unobserved mechanistic latent states from indirect observations. While classical approaches, including expectation maximization, do not scale to combinatorially large spaces, deep learning approaches such as variational autoencoders typically form artificial latent states rather than reconstructing the mechanistic ground-truth states. Here, we introduce GReinSS, a policy learning framework that uses dynamically rescaled rewards to learn latent state distributions that maximize the observed data likelihood. We show that GReinSS accurately reconstructs simulated latent sets and latent graphs, outperforming alternative policy learning and generative modeling baselines. Additionally, GReinSS reconstructs isoforms from real short-read RNA sequencing data that better match isoforms detected by orthogonal long-read sequencing than the standard RSEM algorithm. Overall, GReinSS is a principled and practically effective approach for generative modeling and inference of combinatorial latent states from indirect observations.

</details>


### [146] [Reversible Foundations: Training a 120B Sparse MoE through State-Preserving Scaling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07404)
*Rohan Shravan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种在单节点八GPU上训练千亿级稀疏专家混合模型的方法，通过可逆递归、状态保持增长和单节点经济学三大技术，实现从1.78B到120B的模型规模化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有大规模语言模型训练需要大规模GPU集群，计算成本高昂。研究者希望探索在单节点（8 GPU）上实现大模型训练的可能性，降低训练门槛，同时保持模型能力。

Method: 采用三种核心技术：1）可逆性：使用可逆递归栈在反向传播时重构激活值，使激活内存保持恒定；2）状态保持增长：从密集种子模型逐步扩展为MoE模型，遵循可复现原则；3）单节点经济学：采用TQP策略，量化基础专家权重并训练低秩适配器，将优化器状态从100B+参数降至2.26B。

Result: 成功训练出LightningLM 0.1V模型家族，从1.78B密集模型逐步增长到120B模型（460个路由专家，top-12路由），激活参数仅5.93B（约占总存储参数的5%），最终训练损失为1.78，在单节点上实现8K上下文训练，优化器状态减少约45倍。

Conclusion: 证明了通过整合已知技术原语，可以在单节点上实现千亿参数模型端到端训练，降低了大模型训练门槛。模型家族、分词器和训练代码已开源，为实践者提供了可复现的经验报告。

Abstract: This paper reports on training a hundred-billion-parameter sparse mixture of experts on a single eight-GPU node, end to end. LightningLM 0.1V is a recurrence-backbone language model family grown in four stages from a small dense seed, through a 5B and a 9B mixture of experts, to a 120B model with 460 routed experts under top-12 routing. Each larger model is grown from the trained weights of the smaller one; active parameters rise monotonically from 1.78B at the dense seed to 5.93B at 120B (about 5% of the 118.67B stored). The full lineage runs on single nodes, the larger stages at 8K context, reaching a released training loss of 1.78 at 120B scale.
  This is a systems and experience report. It is organized around three disciplines. Reversibility: a reversible recurrence stack reconstructs activations in the backward pass instead of storing them, holding activation memory flat as the model grows. State-preserving growth: each expansion (dense to MoE, shallow to deep, few experts to many) is given as a reproducible principle paired with the failure that results from getting it wrong; several failures are silent. Single-node economics: the 120B trains through TQP, a strategy of quantized base expert weights and trained low-rank adapters that carries optimizer state on 2.26B adapter parameters rather than 100B+ resident in routed experts, cutting expert-path optimizer state by a factor of ~45.
  What is new is the integration of known primitives, not any primitive in isolation: one grown lineage running end to end on a single node, documented at practitioner level, with per-domain held-out loss as evidence that targeted capabilities (multilingual Indic competence, code) were learned by construction. Model family, tokenizer, and training code are released.

</details>


### [147] [Sparsely gated tiny linear experts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07414)
*Simon Schug*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了稀疏门控线性神经元(sgatlin)，通过将每个专家缩减为单个神经元并移除非线性激活函数，在降低计算成本的同时提升了语言模型的性能和可解释性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 尽管混合专家(MoE)模型的稀疏性不断提高，但单个专家通常仍然庞大且稠密，计算成本仍然较高。作者希望探索进一步的稀疏化是否能在保持或提升模型性能的同时提高计算效率和可解释性。

Method: 作者提出了sgatlin方法：(1)将每个专家缩减为单个神经元，仅选择众多可用神经元中的极小部分；(2)关键性地移除了通常应用于专家的非线性激活函数，形成一个稀疏门控线性神经元网络；(3)用sgatlin替换transformer中所有前馈层进行实验。

Result: 在等计算量(isoflop)比较中，sgatlin在不同计算预算下均改善了语言模型的困惑度。前馈电路形成语义结构化簇，并因果性地参与事实回忆。可解释性方面，无需训练额外的替换模型即可解释sgatlin中的前馈电路。

Conclusion: 本研究为构建计算高效且可解释的transformer前馈层提供了一条可行路径，证明了增加稀疏性（通过缩小专家规模和移除非线性）可以同时提升计算效率和模型可解释性。

Abstract: Sparsity allows scaling model parameters without proportionally increasing computational cost. While mixture of experts (MoE) models are made increasingly sparse, individual experts typically remain large and dense. Here, we demonstrate that further increasing sparsity by shrinking each expert to consist of a single neuron and selecting a tiny fraction of many available neurons can improve compute efficiency and interpretability. Counterintuitively, the key to achieving both is removing the nonlinearity typically applied to the experts, resulting in a network of sparsely gated linear neurons (sgatlin). In an isoflop comparison, we find that replacing all transformer feedforward layers with sgatlin improves perplexity in language models across different compute budgets. At the same time, the sparsity and linearity of the resulting feedforward circuits present new opportunities for model interpretability. In a small-scale case study, we demonstrate that feedforward circuits in sgatlin can be interpreted without having to train additional replacement models. We find that they form semantically structured clusters and are causally implicated in factual recall. Our findings paint a possible path towards compute-efficient and interpretable transformer feedforward layers.

</details>


### [148] [Video-Based Prediction of In-Flight Particle Characteristics in Atmospheric Plasma Spraying](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07416)
*Abhijeet Praveen,Sareh Soleimani,Cormac Cureton,Aman Sidhu,Kintak Raymond Yu,Cristian Cojocaru,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本研究开发了基于等离子体羽流高速视频的非侵入式诊断方法，用于预测大气等离子体喷涂(APS)中的飞行粒子温度和速度。通过比较多种机器学习方法，预训练CNN模型表现最佳，温度和速度预测的R²分别达到0.90和0.82，为APS实时过程监控提供了可扩展的解决方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大气等离子体喷涂(APS)工艺中，飞行粒子的温度和速度对涂层质量有重要影响。然而，这些粒子特性在操作过程中难以连续监测，这推动了非侵入式数据驱动诊断方法的开发需求。传统的监测方法存在局限性，因此需要寻找一种基于视频观察的可靠、可扩展的替代方案。

Method: 研究者引入三种不同的视频衍生特征表示方法，并使用多种模型进行评估：(1) 表格先验数据拟合网络(TabPFN)；(2) 卷积神经网络(CNN)；(3) 经典回归基线模型（随机森林、梯度提升、支持向量回归、XGBoost）。实验采用分组留一交叉验证，基于63次APS喷涂运行的126个标记的前后喷涂视频记录。此外，还评估了直接基于原始视频帧的预训练CNN模型。

Result: 实验结果显示：在工程特征实验中，TabPFN在温度预测方面表现最一致，使用组合特征表示时达到R²=0.86；CNN模型在速度预测方面表现更强，R²达到0.81。直接使用原始视频帧的预训练CNN配合回归头表现最优，温度和速度预测的R²分别达到0.90和0.82，证明了视频衍生的羽流信息为非侵入式APS诊断提供了有前景的基础。

Conclusion: 研究表明，基于高速视频的等离子体羽流信息可以有效地预测APS过程中的飞行粒子特性，为非侵入式APS诊断和实时过程监控提供了有前景且可扩展的基础。这种方法具有实际应用潜力，可改善APS工艺的质量控制和过程优化。

Abstract: Atmospheric plasma spraying (APS) is a widely used coating process in which in-flight particle temperature and velocity strongly influence coating quality. However, these particle characteristics are difficult to monitor continuously during operation, motivating the development of non-invasive data-driven diagnostic methods. In this work, we investigate the predictive potential of high-speed video observations of the plasma plume for estimating in-flight particle characteristics in APS. We introduce three different video-derived feature representations and evaluate them using Tabular Prior-Data Fitted Networks (TabPFN), convolutional neural networks (CNN), and classical regression baselines including Random Forest, Gradient Boosting, Support Vector Regression, and XGBoost. Experiments are conducted using grouped leave-one-out cross-validation on 126 labeled pre- and post-spray video recordings from 63 APS spray runs. Across the engineered feature experiments, TabPFN achieves the most consistent performance for temperature prediction, reaching R2 = 0.86 using the combined feature representation. CNN models particularly perform stronger for velocity prediction, achieving R2 of 0.81. In addition, we evaluate models operating directly on raw video frames using pretrained CNNs and find that the highest performance is achieved by a pretrained CNN with a regression head with R2 of 0.90 and 0.82 for temperature and velocity, respectively. The results demonstrate that video-derived plume information provides a promising and scalable foundation for non-invasive APS diagnostics and real-time process monitoring.

</details>


### [149] [Discovering Multiscale Deep Formulas in Complex Systems via Neural-Guided Lambda Calculus](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07426)
*Hanqiao Yu,Shusen Yang,Xuebin Ren,Cong Zhao*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出了Deflex方法，用于自动提取多尺度复杂系统中的数学公式，包括不变量和分布。该方法在六个复杂系统中实现了比现有方法高达7倍的效率提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 当前基于AI的方法在单尺度系统中表现出色，但在识别多尺度复杂系统中的尺度特定公式方面存在局限性。科学中一个基本问题是以简洁数学公式的形式识别复杂系统的底层模式。

Method: Deflex包含两个子系统：Deflexpressor（用于高阶公式的λ演算符号回归模型）和Deflexformer（用于学习跨尺度统一表示的可分解深度能量模型）。Deflexpressor生成合成数据预训练Deflexformer，后者通过解耦多尺度潜在关系来指导公式发现。

Result: 在六个具有不同行为的代表性复杂系统中，Deflex实现了比最先进方法高达7倍的效率提升，同时实现了自动化多尺度发现。

Conclusion: 该工作可以成为跨学科科学发现的有用工具，为多尺度复杂系统的公式发现提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: A fundamental problem in science is identifying underlying patterns of complex systems in the form of concise mathematical formulas. Current Artificial Intelligence (AI)-based methods have shown strong performance in single-scale systems, yet remain limited in identifying scale-specific formulas in multiscale complex systems. We present Deflex, an end-to-end AI method to automatically extract multiscale formulas with potentially different forms, including invariants and distributions, from complex systems. Deflex consists of two subsystems named Deflexformer and Deflexpressor. Deflexpressor is a lambda-calculus symbolic regression model for higher-order formulas. Deflexformer is a decomposable deep energy model for learning unified representations across scales. Deflexpressor generates synthetic data to pre-train Deflexformer, which then guides formula discovery by decoupling multiscale latent relationships. Across six representative complex systems with diverse behaviors, Deflex achieves up to 7-fold higher efficiency than the state-of-the-art methods while enabling automated multiscale discovery. Our work could be a useful tool for scientific discovery across disciplines.

</details>


### [150] [Time series Foundation Models based on Physics-Informed Synthetic Histories for Cold-Start Photovoltaic Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07457)
*Lorenzo Longarini,Alessandro Rongoni,Simone Silenzi,Emanuele Frontoni,Riccardo Rosati*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出零样本流程解决光伏电站投产期无历史数据的冷启动预测问题，通过合成历史数据使时序基础模型实现预测，性能超越传统基线约1.7-2倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 光伏电站在投产调试阶段缺乏目标站点观测数据，导致标准监督预测方法无法直接应用，需要解决这种冷启动场景下的发电量预测问题。

Method: 提出零样本流程：利用电站元数据和气象协变量生成合成生产历史，通过推理时条件化使时序基础模型（TSFM）能够进行预测。对五种TSFM模型进行基准测试，采用三种策略：严格冷启动基线、真实反馈和自预测反馈。

Result: 在跨四个数据集、440个光伏站点和多种气候区域的评估中，协变量感知基础模型性能超越基线约1.7-2倍。TabPFN-TS在真实反馈策略下取得最低误差（MAE 0.514，RMSE 0.721 kWh kWp⁻¹ d⁻¹），Chronos-2在自预测反馈下最稳健。性能对合成历史数据源不敏感，准确性主要取决于合理时序上下文的可用性。

Conclusion: 所提方法成功解决了光伏电站冷启动预测问题，时序基础模型结合合成历史数据可显著提升预测性能，且对合成数据生成器选择具有鲁棒性，为实际投产期的发电量预测提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: At commissioning time, Photovoltaic (PV) operators must forecast production before target-site observations are available, limiting the direct use of standard supervised forecasters. This cold-start setting is addressed with a zero-shot pipeline that generates a synthetic production history from plant metadata and meteorological covariates, enabling time-series foundation models (TSFMs) to forecast through inference-time conditioning. Five TSFMs are benchmarked against classical baselines under strict Cold-Start Baseline, Real Feedback, and Self-Forecast Feedback strategies. The evaluation spans $440$ PV sites across four datasets and diverse climate regimes. Covariate-aware foundation models outperform baselines by approximately $1.7-2\times$: TabPFN-TS achieves the lowest error under Real Feedback (MAE $0.514$, RMSE $0.721$ $kWh$ ${kWp}^{-1}$ ${d}^{-1}$), while Chronos-2 is most robust under Self-Forecast Feedback. Performance is largely insensitive to the synthetic-history source, indicating that accuracy is driven more by the availability of plausible temporal context than by the specific generator.

</details>


### [151] [Unsupervised Continual Clustering via Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07474)
*Mohammadreza Sadeghi,Sareh Soleimani,Zihan Wang,Narges Armanfard*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出无监督持续聚类（UCC）新任务设置，并创新性地设计了前向-后向知识蒸馏持续聚类方法（FBCC）。该方法通过双阶段蒸馏过程，在无需存储历史数据的前提下，有效缓解了灾难性遗忘问题，在多个基准数据集上显著提升了聚类准确性和知识保留能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无监督持续学习面临灾难性遗忘的核心挑战，而在无标签引导的情况下该问题更加严峻。现有缓解策略（如知识蒸馏和回放缓冲区）存在内存和隐私问题，且当前UCL方法忽视了聚类特定的目标函数。因此，迫切需要一种既能保持聚类结构又能保护隐私的无监督持续学习解决方案。

Method: 提出前向-后向知识蒸馏持续聚类（FBCC）框架，核心架构包括：1）持续教师网络配备聚类投影器；2）轻量级任务特定学生网络；3）创新的双阶段前向-后向蒸馏机制。教师网络通过前向蒸馏学习新聚类，通过后向蒸馏保持已发现的聚类结构，实现无需存储历史数据的增量学习。

Result: 在四个基准数据集上的实验验证表明：FBCC在聚类准确性方面持续优于现有的持续学习基线方法；显著降低了灾难性遗忘程度；成功实现了无监督持续聚类的有效知识保留，无需存储过往数据即可保持先前发现的聚类结构。

Conclusion: FBCC作为无监督持续聚类领域的开创性方法，有效填补了该研究空白。该方法不仅解决了隐私和内存约束问题，还通过双阶段蒸馏机制实现了新知识学习与旧知识保持的平衡，为无监督持续学习在聚类任务中的应用提供了新的技术路径和研究方向。

Abstract: Unsupervised Continual Learning (UCL) aims to enable neural networks to learn sequential tasks without labels or access to past data. A major challenge in this setting is Catastrophic Forgetting, where models forget previously learned tasks upon learning new ones. This challenge is amplified in UCL due to the absence of labels to guide learning and memory retention. Existing mitigation strategies, such as knowledge distillation and replay buffers, often raise memory and privacy concerns. Moreover, current UCL methods largely overlook clustering-specific objectives. To fill this gap, we introduce Unsupervised Continual Clustering (UCC) and propose Forward-Backward Knowledge Distillation for Continual Clustering (FBCC). FBCC employs a continual teacher network with a clustering projector and lightweight task-specific students. Through a dual-phase forward-backward distillation process, the teacher learns new clusters while preserving previously discovered cluster structure without storing past data. FBCC represents a pioneering approach to UCC, demonstrating improved clustering performance across sequential tasks. Experiments on four benchmark datasets demonstrate that FBCC consistently outperforms existing continual learning baselines in clustering accuracy while significantly reducing catastrophic forgetting.

</details>


### [152] [Drifting Models for Surrogate Flow Modeling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07481)
*Chris R. Jung,Markus Dörr,Natalie Jüngling,Jennifer Niessner,Adam T. Müller,Nicolaj C. Stache*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了条件生成漂移框架，在保持高精度的同时实现比扩散模型快两个数量级的单步流场生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 计算流体动力学(CFD)虽能提供高保真流场，但计算成本高昂；现有生成代理模型虽优于确定性网络，但迭代采样速度慢，限制了快速探索能力。

Method: 将生成漂移框架引入流体力学，在VAE潜在空间中进行漂移，并采用标签感知掩码技术使生成样本与边界条件对齐。提出条件架构（包含标签条件和空间条件两种变体），实现单步生成。

Result: 所提出的标签条件模型在精度和流场一致性方面与迭代扩散模型相当，但推理速度快两个数量级。空间条件变体展现出对未见几何形状的泛化潜力。

Conclusion: 条件漂移作为扩散方法的高效替代方案，能够在推理速度至关重要的场景中实现实时CFD代理建模，为室内环境优化提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: While Computational Fluid Dynamics (CFD) provides high-fidelity flow fields for optimizing indoor environments, its computational cost limits rapid exploration. To solve this problem generative surrogates offer better distribution modeling than deterministic networks, but iterative sampling is slow. To enable high-quality, single-pass generation, we adapt the novel generative drifting framework to fluid mechanics. We introduce a conditional architecture that performs drifting in a learned VAE latent space and uses label-aware masking to align generated samples with their boundary conditions. Our label-conditioned model matches iterative diffusion in accuracy and flow consistency while running two orders of magnitude faster. Additionally, we propose a spatial-conditioning variant that establishes a promising path towards generalization to unseen geometries. Ultimately, conditional drifting serves as a highly efficient alternative to diffusion based approaches, unlocking real-time CFD surrogates where inference speed is critical.

</details>


### [153] [Network Recovery from Cascade Data: A Debiased Jacobian-Based Machine Learning Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07483)
*Lei Huang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 提出CascadeNet框架，无需指定扩散机制即可从级联数据中恢复隐藏影响网络，通过Jacobian和Neyman正交去偏实现统计推断，在模拟和COVID-19传播实证中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有网络恢复方法依赖特定扩散模型假设，当假设错误时性能显著下降。而现实中许多重要现象（产品采用、疾病传播、金融困境、信息扩散）都以动态级联形式展开，亟需不依赖特定扩散机制的网络恢复方法。

Method: 提出Jacobian方法：利用一步转移函数的Jacobian表征底层影响结构；构建灵活的转移函数估计器；应用基于Riesz表示子的Neyman正交去偏技术，使去偏Jacobian具有√n一致性和渐近正态性，从而支持对网络结构的形式化统计推断。

Result: 模拟实验中：在9种常见数据生成过程下，CascadeNet的网络恢复准确率最高；实证应用中：在西班牙52个省的COVID-19传播研究中，CascadeNet恢复的传播网络与真实的省际流动网络显著相关，而基线方法恢复的网络与真实情况无显著关联。

Conclusion: CascadeNet成功实现了模型无关的网络恢复，突破了现有方法对扩散模型假设的依赖，为级联数据的网络推断提供了稳健有效的解决方案，具有重要的理论价值和实际应用意义。

Abstract: Many important outcomes unfold as dynamic cascades, including product adoption, disease spread, financial distress, and information diffusion. A central challenge is to recover the hidden influence network behind these cascades. Existing methods typically assume a specific diffusion model, and their performance degrades substantially when that assumption is misspecified. We propose CascadeNet, a Jacobian-based machine learning framework for network recovery that does not require specifying a diffusion mechanism. The key idea is that the underlying influence structure can be characterized by the Jacobian of the one-step transition function. CascadeNet first constructs a flexible estimator of the transition function, and further applies Neyman-orthogonal debiasing via the Riesz representer, so that the debiased Jacobian is $\sqrt{n}$-consistent and asymptotically normal, enabling formal inference on the network structure. We validate CascadeNet in both a simulation exercise and a real-world empirical application. In simulations, where the data-generating process is known, CascadeNet achieves the highest network recovery accuracy across nine common data-generating processes. In an empirical application to COVID-19 transmission across Spain's 52 provinces, CascadeNet recovers transmission networks that are significantly correlated with the true inter-province mobility network, whereas networks recovered by baseline methods show no significant alignment with the ground truth.

</details>


### [154] [CoMetaPNS: Continually Meta-learning Personalized Neural Surrogates for Cardiac Electrophysiology Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07488)
*Ryan Missel,Xiajun Jiang,Linwei Wang*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种持续元学习框架，用于个性化神经心脏模拟器，能够在持续整合新信息的同时识别数据来源，有效解决了灾难性遗忘问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的个性化虚拟心脏模拟方法在模型个性化和计算成本方面面临挑战。虽然神经代理模型提供了先进的解决方案，但它们通常只能解决高效个性化或训练泛化模型中的一项。最新的方法通过少样本生成建模和元学习来学习个性化过程，但这些方法假设训练分布是静态且任务标识已知。当新数据到达时，它们需要使用所有历史数据进行昂贵的重新训练，以避免灾难性遗忘（即模型在学习新任务时忘记早期任务）。这在临床环境中是一个主要限制，因为未标记数据通常是顺序到达的，完全重新训练是不可行的。

Method: 本文提出了一个新的持续元学习框架，用于实现个性化神经代理模型。该框架的核心是在内存缓冲器上利用持续贝叶斯高斯混合模型，能够随时间推断数据的标识符和关系，这对于有效的元学习是必需的。该框架不仅能够持续整合信息，还能够识别传入数据是来自已知还是未知的动力学源。

Result: 在合成心脏数据上的实验结果表明，与现有基线方法相比，该框架在模拟预测、计算可扩展性和对灾难性遗忘的抵抗能力方面表现出优越性。

Conclusion: 所提出的持续元学习框架成功解决了个性化神经心脏模拟器在面对顺序到达的新数据时的适应性问题，无需昂贵的完全重新训练即可持续学习，并能有效识别数据来源，为临床环境中的心脏模拟提供了一个更实用的解决方案。

Abstract: Personalized virtual heart simulations face challenges in model personalization and computational cost. While neural surrogates offer state-of-the-art solutions, they typically address either efficient personalization or training generalizable models. Recent work reframes this by learning the process of personalizing a surrogate using limited subject-specific context data, through few-shot generative modeling with set-conditioned surrogates and meta-learned amortized inference. These methods, however, assume a static and diverse training distribution with known task identifiers. When new data becomes available, they require costly retraining with all prior data to avoid catastrophic forgetting - a phenomena where the model forgets earlier tasks when trained on new ones. This is a major limitation in clinical settings where often unlabeled data arrives sequentially and full retraining is infeasible. This paper presents a new continual meta-learning framework to achieve personalized neural surrogates able to not only continually integrate information but also identify whether incoming data stems from a known or unknown dynamics source. By leveraging a continual Bayesian Gaussian Mixture Model over a memory buffer, our framework can infer the identifiers and relationships of data over time - required for effective meta-learning. Empirical results on synthetic cardiac data demonstrate superior simulation forecasting, computational scalability, and resilience to catastrophic forgetting compared to existing baselines.

</details>


### [155] [Second-Order Path Kernel Interpolation Formulas in Machine Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07495)
*Jin Guo,Roy Y. He,Jean-Michel Morel*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文发展了神经网络预测的二阶插值公式，在Domingos一阶路径核公式基础上增加了曲率加权项，并针对随机梯度下降及其动量变体进行了扩展，建立了终端预测的集中估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解训练数据如何影响神经网络预测是学习理论的核心问题。Domingos在2020年提出的一阶插值公式虽然刻画了模型预测，但仅依赖路径核的一阶特征，无法捕捉优化过程的更精细结构，因此需要发展更高阶的表示理论。

Method: 通过推导二阶泰勒展开形式的插值公式，在原有路径核插值基础上引入曲率加权插值项；针对SGD分析采样诱导的梯度噪声协方差与预测曲率的耦合效应；扩展到带动量的SGD情形，分析记忆因子对插值权重的影响；建立终端预测的集中不等式估计。

Result: 证明了二阶插值公式由主导路径核项和曲率加权项组成；对于SGD，额外出现了采样诱导分量，耦合了预测曲率与小批量梯度噪声的协方差；对于带动量的SGD，插值结构得以保持但权重被记忆相关因子修正；建立了终端预测的集中估计，刻画了围绕期望二阶表示的波动尺度。

Conclusion: 这些结果为神经网络预测的路径核解释提供了精细化理论，二阶插值公式更完整地刻画了优化动力学对模型预测的影响，为理解训练数据与模型泛化性能的关系提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Understanding how training data shape neural network predictions is a central problem in modern learning theory. In 2020, Pedro Domingos proposed an interpolation formula valid for every model learned by deterministic gradient descent. It expresses the model's prediction as an integral, along the optimization path, of a data-dependent kernel that aligns the model's gradients at the test and training data. Such a first-order characterization remains valid for models trained with batch-based stochastic optimization. In this paper, we develop second-order forms of these interpolation formulas. We show that the leading path-kernel interpolation is supplemented by a curvature-weighted interpolation term. For stochastic gradient descent, an additional sampling-induced component appears, coupling the curvature of the prediction with the covariance of mini-batch gradient noise. We also extend the representation to stochastic gradient descent with momentum, where the interpolation structure is preserved but with the weights modified by a memory-related factor. Moreover, we establish a concentration estimate for the terminal prediction, identifying the fluctuation scale around the expected second-order representation. Together, these results provide a refinement of the path-kernel interpretation of neural network prediction.

</details>


### [156] [Accelerated Decentralized Stochastic Gradient Descent for Strongly Convex Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2606.07496)
*Ming Sun,Kun Yuan*

Main category: cs.LG

TL;DR: 本文提出了Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD (MG-ADSGD)算法，首次在去中心化随机优化中同时实现了条件数κ和网络谱间隙1-β的加速依赖，达到了目前最优的通信复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在去中心化随机强凸优化问题中，确定性方法已能同时实现条件数κ和网络谱间隙1-β的加速依赖（即√κ和1/√(1-β)），但现有随机方法无法同时获得这两种改进。这一差距促使研究者设计能够兼顾两者优势的随机算法。

Method: 提出MG-ADSGD算法，核心思想是将gossip深度与mini-batch大小进行耦合。算法结合了Nesterov类型的原始-对偶外推与多轮快速gossip平均，使得额外的通信轮次能够同时改善共识精度并降低梯度方差。

Result: MG-ADSGD达到了通信复杂度Õ(σ²/(μnε)·log(1/ε) + √(κ/(1-β))·log(1/ε))，其中ε为目标精度，n为节点数，σ²为梯度方差。该界在去中心化随机强凸优化问题中达到了目前已知的最优通信复杂度（忽略对数因子）。

Conclusion: 本文成功填补了去中心化随机优化与确定性方法在加速性能上的差距，所提算法首次同时实现了条件数和网络谱间隙的双重加速依赖，为大规模网络学习提供了更高效的通信协议。

Abstract: Decentralized stochastic optimization is a fundamental paradigm for large-scale learning over networks, where agents communicate only with their neighbors and no central coordinator is required. For strongly convex problems, communication efficiency is mainly determined by the condition number \(κ=L/μ\) and the network spectral gap \(1-β\). Although deterministic decentralized methods can simultaneously achieve accelerated \(\sqrtκ\) and \(1/\sqrt{1-β}\) dependences, no existing stochastic method attains both improvements at once. In this paper, we propose \emph{Multi-Gossip Accelerated DSGD} (MG-ADSGD), a decentralized stochastic algorithm that combines Nesterov-type primal--dual extrapolation with multi-round fast gossip averaging. The key idea is to couple the gossip depth with the mini-batch size so that additional communication rounds simultaneously improve consensus accuracy and reduce gradient variance. We show that MG-ADSGD achieves the communication complexity \[ \widetilde{\mathcal O}\!\left( \frac{σ^2}{μnε}\log\frac{1}ε +
\sqrt{\fracκ{1-β}}\log\frac{1}ε \right), \] where \(ε\) denotes the target accuracy, \(n\) is the number of nodes, and \(σ^2\) is the gradient variance. To the best of our knowledge, this bound yields the best currently available communication complexity for decentralized stochastic strongly convex optimization, up to logarithmic factors that are independent of $ε$.

</details>
